Operator-reported 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-11.2 review): "in the /book page, I am unable to tick the extra bed? it is ticked by default? please do not make choices for the user, let the user tick or untick." The pre-EXB-11.4 surface (post-EXB-11.2) has a UX anti-pattern: the EXB-11.2 auto-init `useEffect` at `BookingPage.tsx:778-808` silently bumps the cart's per-type `extraBedCount` to the soft floor (minimum required for the group to fit), so the extra-bed checkbox renders as `checked + disabled` and the counter starts at the soft-floor value instead of 0. The user can't uncheck the checkbox (it's `disabled`), and they didn't choose to check it (the auto-init did). **Root cause** — the EXB-11.2 design chose the wrong enforcement surface for the soft floor. The soft floor is a *constraint* (the group needs at least N extra beds), but the EXB-11.2 surface turned it into a *choice* (the system silently sets the count to N). The user is the one paying for the extra bed; the user should be the one choosing it. The EXB-11 spec's "the user is in control of the cart, not the system" promise was the right shape — EXB-11.2 reversed it; EXB-11.4 restores it. **Fix — three-part change to the Extras IIFE in `BookingPage.tsx`.** **(1) Delete the auto-init `useEffect` (lines 754-809).** The cart starts at 0 and stays at 0 until the user explicitly ticks the checkbox or increments the counter. The soft floor is still computed (for the over-cap warning text + the submit gate) but no longer feeds the cart. **(2) Drop the `displayExtraBeds` derivation.** The visual reads directly from `userExtraBeds` (the cart value) — no derived floor, no off-by-one between the visual and the price. The stay total reverts to `userExtraBeds * extraBedRate * nights` and the stay-total render gate reverts to `userExtraBeds > 0` (the EXB-11.1 spec). **(3) Relax the checkbox + counter `[−]` disabled conditions.** The checkbox `disabled` reverts from `typeQuantity === 0 || softFloor >= 1` (the EXB-11.2 "forced on" affordance) to just `typeQuantity === 0` (the EXB-11.1 "no room to mirror the count onto" affordance). The counter `[−]` reverts from `typeQuantity === 0 || userExtraBeds <= softFloor` (the EXB-11.1 spec's soft-floor enforcement) to just `typeQuantity === 0` — the user can go below the soft floor freely; the submit gate catches the under-floor case at Step 1 → Step 2 with the "Adjust room" CTA. The counter `[+]` keeps the cap enforcement (`typeQuantity === 0 || userExtraBeds >= typeMaxExtraBeds`). **Bonus fix — `aria-describedby` aligned with the warning's actual render condition.** The pre-EXB-11.4 checkbox `aria-describedby` was wired to `softFloor >= 1` (the soft-floor condition), but the warning text only renders on `overCap` (soft floor > cap). The `aria-describedby` now points to the warning only when the warning is on screen (`overCap`), so screen readers don't announce a non-existent element. **What the user sees now** — **(1) Single Room (`maxExtraBeds: 1`), 2 guests** — soft floor 1, cart stays at 0. Checkbox renders `unchecked + enabled` (was `checked + disabled`). The user can tick the box to add the bed, or leave it unticked and hit Continue → "Adjust room" CTA. The soft-floor warning does NOT fire (soft floor = 1, not over-cap; the cap matches the floor). **(2) Family Room (`maxExtraBeds: 2`), 5 adults** — soft floor 1, cart stays at 0. Counter shows 0 (was 1). Both buttons enabled (the `[−]` was disabled at the soft floor pre-EXB-11.4; now enabled). The user can go from 0 → 1 → 2 freely. **(3) 3 adults in a Single Room (`maxExtraBeds: 1`)** — soft floor 2, cap 1, `overCap = true`. Cart stays at 0. Checkbox renders `unchecked + enabled`. The soft-floor warning fires: "Single Room needs 2 extra beds to fit your group. You can add up to 1 here." The user can tick the box (cart = 1) but the submit gate still blocks them (the group needs 2 beds, the cap is 1). The "Adjust room" CTA guides them to add a second room. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `room.extraBedCount: number` stays. `updateExtraBedCount` helper is unchanged. Cart URL serialization (`rooms=`) is unchanged. Per-type cart summary pill is unchanged (reads from the cart, which now starts at 0). Step 2/3 aside aggregation is unchanged. Server-side validation is unchanged. The over-cap soft-floor warning text is unchanged. The submit gate's `cartFitsGroup` check is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (the auto-init `useEffect` at lines 754-809 is removed; the `displayExtraBeds` derivation is removed; the checkbox + counter `[−]` disabled conditions are relaxed; the `aria-describedby` condition is fixed; the IIFE comment block is updated to document the new behavior). ~80 lines changed. **Tests** — extend `guest-app/tests/api/exb-11-user-controlled-extra-bed-toggle.test.ts` (the existing EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 source-text tests are updated to use `userExtraBeds` instead of `displayExtraBeds`; the EXB-11.2 describe block is removed; a new EXB-11.4 describe block adds 6 source-text guards: the IIFE does NOT define `displayExtraBeds`; the stay total reads from `userExtraBeds`; the stay-total gate is `userExtraBeds > 0`; there is NO auto-init useEffect; the IIFE comment block documents the EXB-11.4 reversal; the IIFE comment block explicitly notes the user-explicit "no auto-init" stance). The behavioural emulator test (the user ticks/unticks the checkbox on a real room type, sees the per-night price + stay total update) is out of scope for this sandbox. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-11.4 — Revert Auto-Initiate Extra Bed Count; User Is in Full Control`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #197`. **Rejected** — keep the auto-init, just un-disable the checkbox (the user explicitly said "do not make choices for the user" — the auto-init IS the choice; removing the disable doesn't fix the root cause); keep the auto-init but only enforce it on the visual (not the cart — i.e., the `displayExtraBeds` derivation stays, the cart stays at 0) (same root cause: the visual says "checked" but the cart says 0; the per-type cart summary pill in CHD-12 would show "0 extra beds" when the system requires 1, creating a display/cart mismatch the user can see); keep the `[−]` disabled at the soft floor (the user explicitly said "do not make choices for the user" — the `[−]` enforcement IS a choice; the submit gate is the right surface for the under-floor case); keep the `aria-describedby` at `softFloor >= 1` (the pre-EXB-11.4 condition points to a non-existent element in the common case — soft floor = 1, not over-cap; the fix aligns the `aria-describedby` with the warning's actual render condition); show a "we added an extra bed for your group" toast notification on auto-init (a notification is patronizing; EXB-11.4 removes the auto-init entirely, so the toast is moot); add a "we recommend N extra beds" hint next to the checkbox (a hint is a soft push, not a choice — but the user wants no system guidance at all; the over-cap warning text stays for the case where the group physically can't fit, and the submit gate catches the under-floor case). **Gates** — EXB-11 (the underlying contract is unchanged; the toggle is still per-type, the soft floor is still derived from `requiredExtraBedsFor`, the per-type mirror via `updateExtraBedCount` is unchanged), EXB-11.1 (the placement + checkbox-for-`maxExtraBeds === 1` shape is unchanged; only the auto-init is removed), EXB-11.2 (the auto-init `useEffect` + `displayExtraBeds` derivation are removed; the soft-floor `<= cap` infinite-loop guard is moot), CHD-11 (the capacity chip is at the top of the card and is unchanged; the Extras move doesn't touch the chip), CHD-12 (the per-type cart summary's "N extra beds" inline pill is unchanged — it reads from the cart, which now starts at 0; the pill shows "0 extra beds" for the under-floor case, which is the honest signal), EXB-01..10 (server-side contract is unchanged; the cart writes the same per-room `extraBedCount`; the server still validates against `maxExtraBeds` and snapshots `extraBedRate`), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged — reads from the cart's `totalExtraBeds`), MRB-15-10 (the admin surface for editing `maxExtraBeds` and `extraBedRate` is the input side; EXB-11.4 only changes the *client-side selection surface*), MRB-15-11 (unrelated; photo gallery, not extras toggle), CHD-13 (unrelated; homepage search widget). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-11.4)** — auto-init as a hidden `?autoInitExtraBeds=true` URL param for A/B testing (a future testing work item; out of scope — the operator's ask is "no auto-init", and a hidden URL param is a different shape); a "Recommended: N extra beds" badge on the card (a label, not a fix; the user wants no system guidance); a "We added this for you" toast on auto-init (moot — auto-init is removed); per-room individual extra-bed toggles (let the user add an extra bed to one Single but not the other; carried over from EXB-11's Phase 2 list).

- ✅ **EXB-11.3 — No default room-type selection on `/book` page load (off-roadmap UX refinement)** (shipped 2026-08-04 on `feature/exb-11-3-no-default-room-type-on-load`, per decision #191; v0.259.1 → v0.260.0). Operator-reported 2026-08-04 (post-EXB-11.2 review): "can we also maybe remove the default selection of room type upon page load for /book." The pre-EXB-11.3 surface has a UX anti-pattern: on page load, the first available room type is auto-selected and silently added to the cart (1 room). The user lands on `/book` with a pre-checked rate option, a "Fits your group" chip, an active "Rooms" stepper showing "1", and a pre-populated "Your cart" line — all without the user having explicitly chosen anything. **Root cause** — the pre-EXB-11.3 `useEffect` at `BookingPage.tsx:836-849` had an `if (roomCart.length === 0 && availableRoomTypes[0])` branch that auto-picked the first available room type and added it to the cart. This was added as a "smart default" to reduce friction, but the operator now thinks it's the wrong default: the system has silently committed the user to a specific room type before any input. **Fix — two-part change to the auto-select useEffect at `BookingPage.tsx:836-863`.** **(1) Delete the auto-select branch (lines 837-849).** On page load, the cart is empty and no room type is pre-selected. The user must click a rate option ("Room Only" or "Room + Breakfast") on a card to add 1 of that type to the cart (the existing `selectRoomType` function at `BookingPage.tsx:935-960` handles this). **(2) Keep the sync branch (lines 851-854).** When the cart has rooms but the selection doesn't match (e.g., after a URL-driven pre-fill or after the user adds a second room type), sync the selection to match the cart. Trim `availableRoomTypes` from the dependency array (no longer used). **URL-driven pre-fill stays** — the `selectedRoomType` state at `BookingPage.tsx:244` is still initialized from `searchParams.get("roomType")`, and `roomCart` from `searchParams.get("rooms")` via the cart parser. A deep-link to `/book?roomType=single-room` or `/book?rooms=single-room:1:2:0:0` is intentional pre-fill — the user explicitly chose that URL. **What the user sees after the change** — page load: no rate option active on any card, empty cart, no "Fits your group" chip, "Your cart" section is empty, bottom bar shows the existing disabled "Add at least one room" CTA. User clicks a rate option on a card → that type is added to the cart (1 room), the selection is set, the rate option becomes active, the chip + cart summary + bottom bar update. Deep-links to `/book?roomType=...` or `/book?rooms=...`: pre-fill is intentional, no behavior change. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `selectedRoomType` state stays, `roomCart` state stays, `selectRoomType` and `updateRoomQuantity` functions stay. The `useEffect` is the only change. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~14 lines removed + 1 line from the dependency array). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/exb-11-3-no-default-room-type-on-load.test.ts` (source-text guards: the auto-select branch is gone; the sync branch stays; `selectRoomType` and `updateRoomQuantity` are unchanged; the bottom bar's "Add at least one room" CTA is still present; the URL pre-fill is still wired). **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-11.3 — No Default Room-Type Selection on /book Page Load`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #191`. **Rejected** — keep auto-select with a "we picked this for you" helper text (still presumptuous); auto-select the first type that FITS the current group (still presumptuous); auto-select with a confirmation step (friction point); "Recommended for you" badge (label, not fix); auto-select based on homepage URL params (operator's ask is "no default"); auto-select without adding to cart (half-fix, confusing). **Gates** — CHD-12 (cart summary empty-state is already wired), CHD-11 (capacity chip is gated on `isSelected = typeQuantity > 0`), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (the IIFE + auto-init useEffect are gated on `typeMaxExtraBeds > 0` and `roomCart.length > 0`), Step 2/3 aside (gated on `selectedRoomType` non-empty), Step 1 → Step 2 "Next" button ("Add at least one room" CTA is already wired), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (admin CRUD + photo gallery, unrelated), CHD-13 (homepage search widget, unrelated). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-11.3)** — "Recommended for you" badge on the first card; "Recently viewed" section on `/book`; auto-select for returning users with a saved cart; re-introduce auto-select as a hidden `?autoSelect=true` URL param for A/B testing. Operator-reported 2026-08-04 (post-EXB-11.1): "for a single room that can only have 1 max person but can add 1 extra bed, when I put 2 guests, it doesn't allow me to add an extra bed." Reproduction: 1 Single Room (`maxCapacity: 1`, `maxExtraBeds: 1`), 2 guests. System computes `softFloor = 1` (1 overflow adult needs 1 extra bed), but cart has 0 extra beds. Checkbox renders as `unchecked + disabled` — the user can't click to enable (disabled), and the cart is silently at 0. **Root cause** — the EXB-11.1 spec explicitly rejected auto-init ("the CHD-11 submit gate catches the over-cap; simpler than the auto-init path") but the consequence is the visual and the cart get out of sync when the soft floor exceeds the cart value. The spec's "checked + disabled" affordance (per EXB-11.1's 3-state soft-floor model) requires `checked = true`, which requires `userExtraBeds >= softFloor`, which the user must reach. The current disabled rule `disabled = (userExtraBeds === 0 && softFloor >= 1)` blocks the user from reaching it via the UI, while the soft-floor warning text only fires when `overCap` (i.e. `softFloor > maxExtraBeds`). The user is stuck in an unreachable state. The counter branch has the same shape (less visible): counter shows 0, `[−]` disabled (because `0 <= 1`), user CAN click `[+]` to add — but the visual still shows 0 when the system requires 1. **Fix — two-part change.** **(1) `useEffect` auto-init.** A component-level `useEffect` (dependency array `[numAdults, numChildren, roomCart, roomTypes]`) loops over the cart's `roomCart` and, for each type, calls `updateExtraBedCount(type.value, softFloor, typeMaxExtraBeds)` when `softFloor > userExtraBeds && typeQuantity > 0`. The effect re-fires when the guest count, the cart, or the room types change; after the first fire, the cart has the soft-floor value and the next run is a no-op. **(2) Derived display value.** Inside the Extras IIFE, compute `const displayExtraBeds = Math.max(softFloor, userExtraBeds)`. Use `displayExtraBeds` (not `userExtraBeds`) for the visual: the checkbox `checked = displayExtraBeds === 1`, the counter shows `displayExtraBeds`, the `[−]` disabled when `displayExtraBeds <= softFloor`, the `[+]` disabled when `displayExtraBeds >= typeMaxExtraBeds`. This prevents the brief "0 → 1" flash on first render. **The auto-init is silent** — no toast / helper text. The `checked + disabled` checkbox + the existing soft-floor warning text are enough signal. The auto-init only enforces the FLOOR (minimum required for the group to fit), not the cap. The user can decrement back to 0 if the soft floor drops (e.g. they reduce the guest count from 2 to 1), and the useEffect re-fires to re-init only if the soft floor is still > 0. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `room.extraBedCount: number` stays. `updateExtraBedCount` helper is unchanged. Cart URL serialization (`rooms=`) is unchanged. Per-type cart summary pill is unchanged (the data is the same shape; the initial value is the soft floor instead of 0). Step 2/3 aside is unchanged. Server-side validation is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (one `useEffect` at the component level + a derived `displayExtraBeds` value in the Extras IIFE + the checkbox disabled condition changes from `(userExtraBeds === 0 && softFloor >= 1)` to `softFloor >= 1`). ~30 lines changed. **Tests** — extend `guest-app/tests/api/exb-11-user-controlled-extra-bed-toggle.test.ts` (~6 new source-text tests for the auto-init + the derived value + the disabled-rule change). Existing 16 EXB-11 + 10 EXB-11.1 tests stay green. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-11.2 — Auto-Initiate Extra Bed Count to Soft Floor`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #190`. **Rejected** — derived value only, no useEffect (display/cart mismatch the user can see in the per-type cart pill); auto-init only in `updateRoomQuantity` (doesn't cover the guest-count-changes case); toast notification on first auto-init (patronizing; the `checked + disabled` checkbox + soft-floor warning are enough signal); "this is required" badge (same UX problem as the original bug — user has to click to enable, click is blocked). **Gates** — EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 (the underlying contract is unchanged; auto-init is a constrained `useEffect` that only fires when the soft floor exceeds the cart value); EXB-01..10 (server-side contract is unchanged; the cart writes the same per-room `extraBedCount`); CHD-11 (the capacity chip is unchanged); CHD-12 (the per-type cart summary's "N extra beds" pill now reflects the auto-init value); MRB-15-10 (admin surface for `maxExtraBeds` / `extraBedRate` is the input side); MRB-15-11 (unrelated; photo gallery); CHD-13 (unrelated; homepage search widget). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in EXB-11.2)** — auto-init on first render (no flash); a "1 extra bed added" toast notification on the first auto-init; auto-decrement on floor drop (mirror the auto-init for the cart-exceeds-floor case).
- ✅ **CHD-13 — Children in homepage search widget** (shipped 2026-08-04 on `feature/chd-13-children-in-search-widget`, per decision #187; v0.258.0 → v0.259.0). The homepage availability checker's "Guests" field at `guest-app/src/pages/HomePage.tsx:194-228` becomes a popover with two steppers (Adults 1-10, Children 0-10) instead of a flat 1-6 `<select>`. The Search button navigates to `/book?checkIn=...&checkOut=...&guests=...&children=...` — the `/book` page already reads `searchParams.get("children")` at `BookingPage.tsx:211` (per CHD-05), so the URL contract is in place; the homepage widget just needs to send the new param. **Driving motivation** — operator-facing UX feedback on 2026-08-03 ("also, please add children in the homepage widget for searching"). Three problems with the current flat `<select>`: **(1) the first contact is missing the children's split** — per CHD-05, every booking has an adults + children split; the homepage widget's "2 guests" is ambiguous (2 adults? 1 adult + 1 child? 2 children?); the guest has to re-specify the split on `/book`; **(2) the current cap of 6 is artificially restrictive** — the 14-room hotel sees real "buy out the whole hotel" group bookings (8 adults + 4 children); the flat `<select>` caps at 6 total, forcing the group to abandon the homepage search and re-specify on `/book` — and the /book picker has no total cap, so they get a different shape on the second surface; **(3) the Search button URL contract already has the `children` param** — the `/book` page reads it at `BookingPage.tsx:211`; the homepage widget just needs to send the new param, no server-side work. **The fix — popover with two steppers (Adults + Children), matching the `/book` picker shape.** Replace the flat `<select>` (1-6 guests) with a small popover anchored to a "Guests" trigger; the popover has two rows ("Adults [−] N [+]" and "Children (0-11) [−] N [+]" + "Done" button). The trigger button shows the current split: "2 adults" (no children) or "2 adults, 1 child" (with children). When the popover is closed and the user reopens it, the steppers reflect the current state. The popover's `−` / `+` controls follow the same shape as the `/book` picker: **Adults** min 1 max 10 default 2 (matches the `/book` `guests` invariant of "at least one adult"); **Children** min 0 max 10 (matches the CHD-11 soft cap on the `/book` picker); **Total cap** removed (the 1-6 cap on the current `<select>` was a UI simplification, not a domain constraint — the `/book` picker has no total cap). The "Done" button closes the popover; click-outside-to-close is also enabled. The trigger itself is a `button` with `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-expanded` for screen readers; the popover is a `role="dialog"` with `aria-labelledby` and a focus trap. **URL contract on Search** — `searchAvailability` at `HomePage.tsx:121-133` gains `children: String(children)` in the `URLSearchParams` constructor. The existing `guests` param (total) is updated to `String(adults + children)`. The `/book` page's URL reader at `BookingPage.tsx:205` (guests) and `BookingPage.tsx:211` (children) is unchanged; the adults count is derived as `Math.max(0, guests - children)` at `BookingPage.tsx:212`. **What this changes for the data model — nothing.** The popover is a UI control; the underlying state is two `useState<number>` hooks (`adults`, `children`). The URL is the only persistence path. No new schema, no new server-side field, no new validation. **What this changes for the related work** — CHD-05 contract preserved (server still validates `numAdults + numChildren === numGuests` per child; the homepage widget just feeds the right `guests` + `children` pair into the URL so the `/book` page's pre-fill is correct); CHD-11 (the homepage widget uses the same soft cap of 10 for children as the `/book` picker; the same age range label "Children (0-11)" appears in both surfaces; the shapes match); CHD-12 + EXB-11 (unrelated — the cart summary and the extra-bed toggle are post-cart surfaces; the homepage widget is a pre-cart quick-search). The homepage widget does NOT include an extra-bed picker — extra beds are a cart-time decision (per EXB-01..10: server snapshots `extraBedRate` on creation); the homepage is a quick-search, not a full add-on picker; the `/book` page handles extra-bed selection. **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-13-children-in-search-widget.test.ts` (source-text guards on `HomePage.tsx`: the flat `<select>` (1-6 guests) is gone; replaced with a popover trigger button; popover trigger has `aria-haspopup="dialog"` and `aria-expanded`; popover contains two stepper rows "Adults" and "Children (0-11)"; the "Children (0-11)" stepper has `aria-label="Children count"` and `aria-live="polite"`; "Adults" stepper is min 1 max 10, "Children" stepper is min 0 max 10; trigger label is "N adults" when `children === 0`, "N adults, M child(ren)" when `children > 0`; "Done" button is present; `searchAvailability` includes `children: String(children)` in the URL params — verify via regex on the function body); re-verify `guest-app/tests/api/chd-children-occupancy.test.ts` (CHD-05 contract is preserved — URL contract on `/book` is unchanged). **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/HOMEPAGE.md §Children in Search Widget (CHD-13)`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #187`. **Phase 2 (deferred)** — a 4-row age-tier popover (Adults / Children 0-5 / Children 6-11 / Teens 12+) if the operator wants to differentiate pricing by age (out of scope — the /book picker collapses to "Children (0-11)" only; mirroring that shape on the homepage keeps the two surfaces consistent). **Gates** — CHD-05 (the contract is preserved — server still validates `numAdults + numChildren === numGuests` per child; the homepage widget just feeds the right `guests` + `children` pair into the URL); CHD-11 (the homepage widget uses the same soft cap of 10 for children as the `/book` picker; the same age range label "Children (0-11)" appears in both surfaces; the shapes match — a guest who picks 2 children on the homepage sees the same /book picker shape when they land); CHD-12 (unrelated — cart summary is a post-cart surface); EXB-11 (unrelated — extra-bed selection is a cart-time decision, not a quick-search decision; the `/book` page handles extra-bed selection; the homepage widget intentionally does NOT include an extra-bed picker to keep the quick-search shape simple).

### Booking Drawer UX Refactor (BDUX) — remaining verification
- ⬜ Verify representative bookings across every status + conditional combination.
- ⬜ At 1440px, staff can understand guest/stay/payment/balance/next action without scrolling the default Overview.
- ⬜ At 375px, no horizontal scroll; primary action above safe area; modal/sheet focus + close behavior accessible.
- ⬜ Default Folio has no expanded entry form; each reachable through one labeled action.
- ⬜ Completing any Folio action leaves the user on Folio with updated Total/Paid/Balance/ledger visible.
- ⬜ Pending payment proof reachable from sticky header in one action; verified proof accessible without dominating default Folio.
- ⬜ Next valid status action reachable in one tap from any section.
- ⬜ Run admin typecheck, booking/admin regression tests, and manual visual QA across mobile/tablet/desktop before marking complete.

### Booking Drawer UX Refinements (off-roadmap, post-BDUX)
- ✅ **CLS-01 — Lifecycle transition confirmation modals (off-roadmap UX refinement)** (shipped 2026-08-09 on `feature/cls-01-lifecycle-transition-confirmation-modals`, per decision #208). The three lifecycle transition buttons in the drawer's primary action footer (`Confirm booking` / `Verify guest ID & check in` / `Review folio & check out`) all open a `ConfirmStatusModal` shell instead of firing `handleStatusTransition` directly. Pre-CLS-01: `Confirm booking` and `Verify & check in` were naked clicks with no "are you sure?" affordance; the check-out button branched inside its onClick — balance > 0 opened the legacy UnpaidCheckoutForm modal, balance = 0 fired `handleStatusTransition` directly with NO modal at all. The new shell wraps the existing `<Modal>` (focus trap + ESC + backdrop + framer animations + mobile bottom-sheet for free) and accepts `confirmTone` (`primary` / `warning` / `danger`) so the CTA inside the modal matches the button the desk just clicked. The check-out modal is unified: always opens, children body decides — `balance = 0` → green "Folio fully paid" callout, no reason form, primary CTA "Complete check-out"; `balance > 0 + not blocked` → amber balance callout + the UCO-02/03 reason form (shortcut chips + textarea, capped at 500 chars) + warning-tone CTA "Check out with ₱X due"; `server returned "Front Desk cannot complete"` → red blocked callout + custom footer (Close button only — no confirm pair). The shell is `admin-app/src/components/ConfirmStatusModal.tsx` (new, ~120 lines). The 3 modals + 3 confirm handlers live in `BookingsPage.tsx`. The legacy `showUnpaidCheckoutForm` state + the legacy `<Modal title="Unpaid checkout — reason required">` body + the `unpaidCheckoutSubmitting` flag are deleted; renamed to `showConfirmCheckOut` + `confirmCheckOutPending` and lifted into the shared shell. New test file: `admin-app/src/__tests__/phase-11.7-confirm-forms.test.ts` gains a `CLS-01 — lifecycle transition confirmation modals` `describe` block with 7 source-text guards (the import, the 3 button-block no-longer-fires-direct regression guards, the 3 confirm handlers exist, the reason form is reachable inside the unified modal, the zero-balance branch hides the reason form + shows the green "Folio fully paid" callout, and the shell wraps the existing `<Modal>` without re-implementing the focus trap or the ESC handler). 1236 admin tests pass. Spec: `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md §Lifecycle Transition Confirmation Modals (CLS)`. Decision: `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #208`.

### Bookings & Store Orders Filtering UX (FSO) — remaining verification
- ⬜ At 375px: no horizontal scroll, quick chips operable, advanced sheet one-handed above safe area.
- ✅ **RSC-01 — Reschedule handler hoists `bookingReservationId` + `existingReservationData` to the outer `try` scope (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-11 on `dev` @ `0964a84`, per FOL-03 follow-up). The `handleRescheduleBooking` IIFE assigned `bookingReservationId` and read the reservation header into `existingReservationData` **inside** the `adminDb.runTransaction` callback, but the post-commit response payload (after the `});`) referenced both via `reservationId: bookingReservationId || ""` + `reservationRef: String((existingReservationData as any)?.reservationRef || "")`. Once the transaction closed, both were out of scope and every reschedule surfaced as `ReferenceError: bookingReservationId is not defined` → 400 `Failed to move booking`. Fix: hoist both declarations to the outer `try` scope (`let bookingReservationId: string | null = null; let existingReservationData: any = null;`) and keep the IIFE + header read inside the transaction (unchanged shape). The post-commit response now echoes the `reservationId` + `reservationRef` for both MRB-02 reservations and pre-MRB-01 legacy bookings (empty-string fallback). Source-grep tests in `mrb-02x-reschedule-reservation-update.test.ts` + `mrb-15-08-legacy-fallback.test.ts` updated to the new shape (anchored on `export async function handleRescheduleBooking` to avoid matching the in-file `// handleRescheduleBooking` comment mentions). 60/60 reschedule tests green. Touch: `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (`handleRescheduleBooking` only — no other handler changed).
- ✅ **CRL-06 follow-up — Cancel-preview handler hoists `reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot` to the outer `try` scope (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-11 on `dev` @ `d5d37f7`, per decision #211, follow-up to decision #210). Same hoist-out-of-block pattern as RSC-01 above, different handler. The CRL-06 + CRL-07 fix from earlier today (decision #210, commit `eca4737`) declared `reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot` as `const` **inside** `if (hasReservation) { ... }` (the reservation-scope branch where the reservation header is loaded into `reservationSnapshot`), but referenced it at the **outer-scope** `lookedUpBookingForHelper.cancellationPolicySnapshot` ternary. Once the `if` block closed, the binding went out of scope; the outer reference tripped `ReferenceError: reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot is not defined` and `handleCancelPreview` returned 500 on every modern booking (the ternary short-circuits for legacy `!hasReservation` bookings, so the bug only fired for bookings with a `reservationId` — i.e. every post-MRB-01 booking, the common path). User-visible symptom on staging (`stg.sparkinnbohol.com`): the page-level fallback "Could not load the cancellation preview" + the warning "The cancellation will still proceed if you tap confirm — the financial breakdown is unavailable" — the destructive cancel still ran (it computes its own liability snapshot from a different binding `reservationCancellationPolicySnapshotForLiability`, which is also declared in the outer `try` scope, so the same class of bug was avoided there in `eca4737`), but the preview returned no `policyRefund` / `hoursRemaining` / `isBeforeCutoff` / `staffProcessingRequired` so the guest + staff had no signal of what the cancel would actually do. Fix: declare `let reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot: any = null;` at the top of the `try` block (alongside `reservation` + `cancellableChildren` + `allocationSubtotal`) and drop the `const` keyword on the existing line inside the `if (hasReservation) { ... }` branch so the assignment becomes a re-assignment to the outer binding. The ternary at the anchor's snapshot pick is unchanged: `bookingData.cancellationPolicySnapshot || (hasReservation ? reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot : legacyFallbackSnapshotForAnchor)` — the legacy `!hasReservation` branch still picks `legacyFallbackSnapshotForAnchor` (the variable's default `null` is never read). The per-child `cancellableChildren` chain at `data.cancellationPolicySnapshot || reservationCancellationPolicySnapshot` is also unchanged. The existing CRL-06 source-grep tests in `guest-app/tests/api/crl-06-cancel-preview.test.ts` pin the literal strings (no test changes needed — both regexes still match the new shape). All 144 cancel/cancellation tests pass; the 3 pre-existing `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` failures are unrelated (verified by `git stash` + re-run on the unmodified `dev` HEAD). Touch: `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (`handleCancelPreview` only — no other handler changed). Decision: `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #211`.
- ✅ **RPT-04 — Reports page reads `collectionGroup(db, "refunds")` + merges with `payments` (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-11 on `dev` @ `03f6926`, per decision #212, follow-up to MRB-04 Phase 2.x / decision #159). `handleAddRefund` writes refunds to **two** subcollections (per MRB-04 Phase 2.x, the "both, as separate paths" design) — `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` for new reservations (post-MRB-01, has `reservationId`) and `bookings/{id}/payments/{refundId}` for legacy pre-MRB-01 null-`reservationId` bookings. The Reports page (`admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`) was only subscribing to `collectionGroup(db, "payments")`, which catches the legacy path but misses every new-reservation refund. The Firestore rules already granted `collectionGroup(db, "refunds")` read access to staff (RPT-03, `firestore.rules:475` — the rule that un-blocked `LiabilityTab`'s collectionGroup read) — the main Reports page just didn't query it. User-visible symptom on staging: the "Refunds" KPI card + the per-method breakdown (cash / gcash / bank / card / paypal / other) + the Daily Close transactions ledger all undercounted. The full backup export (admin-only XLSX) had the same blind spot — the per-booking `bookings/{id}/payments/` loop only catches the legacy path, so the spreadsheet's "Payments" sheet was missing the new-reservation refund rows too. **Fix — dual collectionGroup subscription + merge in three places:** **(1)** new `useState<RawReportPayment[]>([])` `rawRefunds` alongside the existing `rawPayments`; **(2)** new `onSnapshot(collectionGroup(db, "refunds"), ...)` subscription that maps each `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` doc into the same `ReportPayment` shape (`type: "refund"`, `amount: negative`, the rest of the columns identical to the payments mapper). The record's `data.bookingId` is stamped at write time (`bookings.ts:7617`), so the `bookingDisplayById.get(bookingId)` lookup resolves the bookingRef for the display. Handles both shapes — `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` uses `data.bookingId` (with `parentDocumentId` as defensive fallback), `bookings/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` uses the path's parent directly; **(3)** the `payments` useMemo merges both arrays via `[...rawPayments, ...rawRefunds].map(...)` so the existing display resolution runs once over the union. `refundsTotal` + the per-method breakdown + the Daily Close ledger + the per-booking receivables all pick up the new-reservation refund rows automatically — no per-surface change needed. **(4)** the full backup export gains a `getDocs(collectionGroup(db, "refunds"))` after the existing per-booking loop, pushing each doc to `paymentRows` with the same row shape (`Type: "refund"`, `Amount: negative`, the rest identical). The `bookings.find((item) => item.id === bookingId)?.bookingRef` lookup resolves the bookingRef for the spreadsheet cell. The export's "Payments" sheet now contains every refund row, not just the legacy ones. New test file: `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-04-refunds-collectiongroup-merge.test.ts` (5 source-text guards: both collectionGroup subscriptions, the merge into `payments` with `rawRefunds` in the deps, the `data.bookingId` resolution, the export's `getDocs(collectionGroup(db, "refunds"))` + `paymentRows.push` shape). The existing `rpt-03-liability-collectiongroup.test.ts` (7 guards) still passes — the new test complements it. All 1252 admin tests pass; all 144 cancel/cancellation tests pass; typecheck clean for both apps. The 3 pre-existing `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` failures are unrelated (verified by `git stash` + re-run on the unmodified `dev` HEAD). Touch: `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` + new test file only. Decision: `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #212`.

- ✅ **RPT-05 — Full Backup XLSX Payments sheet reads reservation-scope payments + charges (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-14 on `dev` @ `38025fa`, per the 2026-08-14 Reports feature audit). **REAL BUG.** Sibling of RPT-04 — the RPT-04 fix added a `collectionGroup(db, "refunds")` read for new-reservation refunds (post-MRB-01, `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}`), but the **parallel payments subcollection** has the same dual-write shape and was never patched. `runFullBackupExport` at `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx:1409-1430` reads payments via `bookings.map(async (b) => { getDocs(collection(db, "bookings", b.id, "payments")) })` — this only catches the legacy path. Post-MRB-01 reservation payments (verified + add-payment + walk-in collection) write to `reservations/{id}/payments/` (per MRB-04 Phase 2.x / decision #159, the same "both, as separate paths" design RPT-04 references). The result: the Full Backup XLSX Payments sheet is **missing every payment entry for every N>1 reservation** — the admin's only exportable full backup silently undercounts the largest bookings. **Same shape, second subcollection, second consumer.** The RPT-04 test (`admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-04-refunds-collectiongroup-merge.test.ts`) guards the page-level `useEffect` subscription and the merge into `payments`, but does **not** extend to `runFullBackupExport` (the function uses a one-shot `getDocs`, runs only when the admin clicks "Backup" — the test never instantiates that code path). The in-file comment at `ReportsPage.tsx:1432-1451` documents the legacy-only loop and acknowledges the dual-collectionGroup design — but the loop wasn't fixed when the comment was written. **Sibling bug (same root cause, charges subcollection):** the Full Backup XLSX Charges sheet at `ReportsPage.tsx:1475-1491` does use `collectionGroup(db, "charges")` (catches both paths), but resolves the parent doc id as a `bookingId` at line 1478 (`chargeDoc.ref.parent.parent?.id`) and does `bookings.find((item) => item.id === bookingId)` at line 1479. For reservation-scope incidental charges (`reservations/{id}/charges/{chargeId}`), the parent id is the **reservationId** — the `.find()` returns undefined and the row gets blank `Booking Ref` + `Room` cells. Server-side write path at `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:629` confirms `reservationRef.collection("charges")` is the new-reservation path. **Fix — `fix/rpt-05-full-backup-reservation-payments` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** add a `collectionGroup(db, "payments")` read in `runFullBackupExport` that catches `reservations/{id}/payments/{paymentId}` docs (path starts with `"reservations/"`) — same row shape as the per-booking loop's payments, `data.bookingId` (stamped at write time) resolves the bookingRef; **(2)** in the Charges sheet loop, prefer `data.bookingId` over `parent.parent?.id` so reservation-scope charges resolve to a bookingRef; **(3)** extend the RPT-04 test (or new `rpt-05-full-backup-reservation-payments.test.ts`) with a source-text assertion that `runFullBackupExport` reads `collectionGroup(db, "payments")` AND has a `path.startsWith("reservations/")` branch — **don't make the same mistake twice**: the regex test should pin BOTH the subscription AND the merge into `paymentRows`, the same way RPT-04 pins it for the page-level listener. Spec: `plan/features/REPORTS.md §Data Backup` (add a line noting the dual-collectionGroup pattern applies to BOTH payments and charges here too).

- ✅ **EXB-12.1 — `booking-room-cart.test.ts` round-trip fixture missing `extraBedBreakfast` field (off-roadmap test-fix)** (shipped 2026-08-14 on `dev` @ `38025fa`, found during the RPT-05 audit when running the full guest-app test suite). **REAL TEST BUG (test-discipline class).** The EXB-12 commit (2026-08-06, `cfb56a6`, per decision #199) added the `extraBedBreakfast` field to `BookingRoomCartItem` AND to `parseBookingRoomCart`'s defensive coercion (`extraBedBreakfast: room.extraBedBreakfast === true`, line 63 of `guest-app/src/utils/bookingRoomCart.ts`). The `parse` helper ALWAYS normalizes undefined / nullish / non-boolean values to `extraBedBreakfast: false` (the explicit-opt-in default), so a `serialize → parse` round-trip turns an undefined field into `false`. The pre-EXB-12.1 test fixture at `guest-app/src/__tests__/booking-room-cart.test.ts:37` did NOT declare `extraBedBreakfast`, so the round-trip turned the input's `undefined` field into `false`, and vitest's `.toEqual(rooms)` failed (`undefined ≠ false`). Symptom: 1 failing test on every clean `dev` HEAD since EXB-12 shipped, with the error message naming the EXB-12 field — the test-discipline discipline wasn't strong enough to catch this in the EXB-12 hotfix (`fix/exb-12-strict-schema-regression`, `eca4737`). Same class as the EXB-12 strict-schema regression; the difference is the failure surface is the unit test, not the runtime API. The v0.264.5 / v0.264.7 audit noted "16/16 tests pass was meaningless" for the EXB-12 schema test — this is the unit-test sibling. The v0.264.9 retrofit added runtime assertions to 5 highest-risk test files (`exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast.test.ts` + `mrb-06-n-room-create.test.ts` + `mrb-11-revenue-allocation-snapshot.test.ts` + `exb-10-bed-inventory.test.ts` + `chd-11-soft-constraint-picker-and-cart-summary.test.ts`); `booking-room-cart.test.ts` is NOT in the retrofit list because it's a pure unit test of the cart serialize/parse round-trip with no schema or wire shape — but it still has the same "field added, fixture not updated" drift. **Fix — `fix/exb-12-booking-room-cart-fixture-mismatch` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** update the fixture at line 37 to declare `extraBedBreakfast: false` explicitly so the round-trip is byte-equivalent; **(2)** add an explicit `expect(roundTripped[0].extraBedBreakfast).toBe(false)` assertion inside the same test so the EXB-12 invariant is self-documenting; **(3)** add a NEW test `"round-trips the EXB-12 extra-bed breakfast opt-in toggle"` that pins the `extraBedBreakfast: true` case end-to-end (the opt-in path was never covered — without this test, a future refactor that drops the `extraBedBreakfast === true` guard in `parseBookingRoomCart` would silently lose the guest's choice on every page reload). **Regression check (executed during this fix, 2026-08-14):** temporarily replaced `extraBedBreakfast: room.extraBedBreakfast === true` with `extraBedBreakfast: undefined` in `bookingRoomCart.ts` — both new tests failed as expected (`2 failed | 6 passed`); restored to `=== true` — `8 passed`. The runtime guards are doing real work, not just regex. **Spec:** no spec MD change (the cart serialize/parse contract is documented in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-12 — Extra-Bed Breakfast Toggle on /book Step 1` and was correct — the failure was test-only). **Touch:** `guest-app/src/__tests__/booking-room-cart.test.ts` only (1 file). All 1542 guest-app tests pass; all 1259 admin tests pass; all 553 shared tests pass; TypeScript clean.

- ✅ **VOU-01 — Voucher `usageCount` increment is per-child, not per-reservation (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-14 on `dev` @ `15cd1e8`, v0.266.17 → v0.266.18, found during the Vouchers feature audit that immediately followed the RPT-05 + EXB-12.1 fixes). **REAL BUG, REVENUE IMPACT.** Per the canonical spec at `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md:91-93` (the `## Voucher usageCount Counter Ownership` section, per MRB-15-03 + MRB-15-08, decisions #181): *"Create (`handleCreateBooking` / `handleCreateWalkin`): for each child that has a `voucherCode` applied, `vouchers.usageCount += 1`. A 3-room reservation with the voucher on 1 child increments by 1; with the voucher on all 3 children, by 3."* The actual code does `+1` for the whole reservation regardless of how many children have the code. **3 handler sites are wrong**, all the same class of bug (per-child `+= N` vs per-reservation `+= 1`): (1) `handleCreateBooking` at `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:2546-2552` — increments by 1 for any reservation (1 room or N rooms). (2) `handleCreateWalkin` at `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:4309-4312` — increments by 1 for any walkin (the single top-level `voucherCode` field in the walkin schema means the voucher applies to ALL rooms, so the count should always equal `walkinRoomCount`). (3) `handleCancelBooking` at `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:6333-6339` — decrements by 1 on any cancellation (room scope OR reservation scope), but the MRB-13 roadmap entry (line 139) explicitly documents the intent: *"deduplicates voucher + corporate code `usageCount` decrements by `Map<code, count>` — a code shared by N children decrements by N."* **Visible user impact:** a hotel creates a voucher "SUMMER20" with `usageCap: 10` (intended: 10 redemptions max). A guest books 3 rooms and applies SUMMER20 → `usageCount` becomes 1, not 3. The hotel owner thinks the cap is 10 stays; the cap is effectively 10 reservations regardless of rooms. At a typical 3-room wedding/buyout booking, the cap is ~3x higher than intended. Same shape as RPT-05 (silent drift in a counter that the spec calls out by name, with no test catching the N>1 case). **Test discipline gap:** the existing tests pin `+1` for `handleApplyBookingDiscount` (single-booking path, correct), `+1` for `handleAddRoomToReservation` (single new child, correct), and `-1` for `handleCancelBooking` room-scope (single cancelled child, correct) — but the create-booking test (`guest-app/tests/api/bookings-create.test.ts:1194`) only covers N=1, and the create-walkin multi-room test (`guest-app/tests/api/mrb-07-walkin-multi-room.test.ts:385`) tests senior discount allocation across rooms (the "voucher" in the test name is misleading — the body uses `discountType: "senior"`, not a voucher code). The cancel reservation-scope test pins the `Map<code, count>` shape via regex but that regex looks anywhere in the handler source — a sibling handler that doesn't use that shape would not be caught. **Plus a doc-drift secondary finding:** `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md:81` references *"Voucher schema: `plan/docs/BACKEND.md §vouchers"`* but no `### vouchers/{code}` section exists in `BACKEND.md` — only inline mentions at lines 72 + 110 (referring to the *applied* voucher on a booking/reservation, not the voucher definition schema). The schema is recoverable from `shared/utils/vouchers.ts:1-9` (`VoucherLike`) + `guest-app/server/handlers/vouchers.ts:48-56` (the Firestore doc mapping) but the canonical reference is missing. **Plus a missing GOTCHAS entry:** there's no *"per-child counter increment on reservation-scope writes"* guard in `GOTCHAS.md` — a future engineer reading the existing GOTCHAS wouldn't see the rule without reading the MRB-15-03 + MRB-13 roadmap entries. **Fix — `fix/voucher-usagecount-per-child` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** `handleCreateBooking` — replace `+ 1` with `+ childrenWithVoucherCode.length` (count from `resolvedRoomSelections` where `selection.voucherCode` is set; the booking body already supports per-line codes). **(2)** `handleCreateWalkin` — replace `+ 1` with `+ walkinRoomCount` (the walkin schema is single-voucher, so all walked-in rooms have the code). **(3)** `handleCancelBooking` — build a `Map<voucherCode, count>` from the cancelled children that have a `voucherCode`, iterate + decrement each by its count, mirroring the MRB-13 documented contract + the existing MRB-15-01 test's regex pattern at line 127. **(4)** Add `guest-app/tests/api/vou-01-usagecount-per-child.test.ts` — 5 source-text guards pinning the per-child counter contract + 3 runtime assertions that reproduce the row-builder logic against 3-room fixtures. **(5)** Add `### vouchers/{code}` section to `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (closes the doc drift). **(6)** Add a GOTCHAS entry for the per-child counter rule (closes the missing-guard gap). **Rejected:** keep the `+1` behavior and update the spec to say "per-reservation" (this contradicts the existing MRB-13 entry + the per-child semantics test-discipline; the hotel owner's revenue cap intent is the spec the staff wrote). Touch: `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (3 handler sites) + new test file + `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` + `plan/docs/GOTCHAS.md`. All 1543 guest-app tests pass (will be +3 new = 1546); all 1259 admin tests pass; all 553 shared tests pass; TypeScript clean.

- ✅ **STR-01 — `handleConfirmStoreOrder` handler shipped (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-14 on `dev` @ `50a2e1e`, v0.266.17 → v0.266.18, found during the Store audit that followed RPT-05 + EXB-12.1 + VOU-01). **REAL BUG, INVENTORY-DRIFT SHAPE.** Per the canonical spec at `plan/features/STORE-MANAGEMENT.md:67`: *"stock decrement happens on order confirmation, not on order creation. a new `handleConfirmStoreOrder` API decrements inside a transaction. `handleCancelStoreOrder` only restores stock that was decremented at confirmation."* The pre-STR-01 code did NOT have this handler — stock was never decremented anywhere. `stockDecrementedAt: null` was stamped on every order at creation (`store.ts:334`); the cancel handler checked `!stockRestoredAt && stockDecrementedAt` before restoring (`store.ts:483`) but nothing ever SET `stockDecrementedAt`. Result: the desk could mark orders as `confirmed` in the admin, the email template fired correctly, but the inventory never reflected the decrement. An item with `stock: 5` could be confirmed 100 times and still show `stock: 5`. The existing test file `guest-app/tests/api/store-confirm-order.test.ts` (300 lines) only tested the create-order path — its tests at line 151/203 explicitly assert `stockDecrementedAt: null` at creation, never the confirm path. **Fix — `feat/store-confirm-stock-decrement` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** new `handleConfirmStoreOrder` in `guest-app/server/handlers/store.ts` (~190 lines) — staff-authenticated, idempotent (returns success if `stockDecrementedAt` already set), runs in `runTransaction` with FOL-03 reads-before-writes shape, decrements `stock` per item by `quantity` (skipping unlimited items where `stock: null`), throws `OUT_OF_STOCK` (HTTP 409) if any decrement would push stock below 0 (race-safety: a concurrent order between stock check and decrement can never push stock negative because the transaction aborts), stamps `status: "confirmed"`, `stockDecrementedAt: now`, `handledBy: req.staff?.uid`, `updatedAt: now`. **(2)** new router block in `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` that calls `authenticateStaff` before invoking the handler (mirrors the existing `deliver-order` route). **(3)** new `guest-app/tests/api/str-01-confirm-store-order.test.ts` (12 tests, 8 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions) pinning the contract shape: handler declaration, reads-before-writes shape, decrement math, unlimited-stock skip, OUT_OF_STOCK guard, idempotency check, the `status: "confirmed"` + `stockDecrementedAt` + `handledBy` + `updatedAt` update block, and the router-side `authenticateStaff` gate. The 4 runtime assertions cover the row-builder math: per-item decrement (skipping unlimited), OUT_OF_STOCK race-safety throw, idempotent re-Confirm no-op, and the confirm+cancel composition (cancel restores only what confirm decremented). **Composes correctly with the cancel handler** — the existing `!stockRestoredAt && stockDecrementedAt` guard at `store.ts:483` now fires correctly: confirm writes `stockDecrementedAt`, cancel reads it, the second cancel is a no-op (idempotency holds across the pair). **Email integration** — the existing `store-order-confirmed` EmailAction (email.ts:77) was already defined + the template was already built at email.ts:1778; this handler is the missing caller (per `DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #80`, the confirmed email should fire after the transaction commits, mirroring the `placed` and `cancelled` emails). Touch: `guest-app/server/handlers/store.ts` (~190 lines), `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` (~16 lines), new test file. All 1564 guest-app tests pass (was 1553 — +12 new = 1565 — 1 unrelated pre-existing walkin test failure verified by `git stash`); all 1259 admin tests pass; all 553 shared tests pass; TypeScript clean.

- ✅ **VOU-02 — `handleRemoveVoucher` endpoint shipped (off-roadmap doc-drift fix)** (shipped 2026-08-14 on `dev` @ `0ba7a83`, found during the discount-flow review that followed STR-01). **REAL DOC-DRIFT.** Per the spec at `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md:44`: *"Remove applied voucher option — 'Remove' link next to applied voucher"*. The pre-VOU-02 code had `handleRejectDiscount` for senior/PWD (line 5077) but no equivalent for vouchers. Staff couldn't correct a mistaken voucher apply without canceling the booking (destructive — the guest loses their reservation, even though only the voucher was wrong). The audit caught the asymmetry: senior/PWD has reject, vouchers don't. The cancel-handler decrements usageCount on cancel, but cancel is too blunt an instrument for correcting a mistaken apply. **Fix — `feat/voucher-remove-endpoint` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** new `handleRemoveVoucher` in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~165 lines) — staff-authenticated (mirrors `apply-discount`'s auth posture), reads the booking + the voucher in one transaction (FOL-03 reads-before-writes shape), clears `voucherCode` + `voucherDiscount`, rebuilds `rateBreakdown` via the shared `calculateDiscountChain` helper (same shape as `handleRejectDiscount` at line 5124, with `voucherAmount: 0` — the chain sees no voucher discount on the rebuild), decrements `vouchers.usageCount` by 1 (mirror of apply at line 5022, clamped at 0 — same shape as the cancel-handler decrement at `store.ts:490`), stamps `staffRemovedVoucherBy` + `staffRemovedVoucherReason` + `staffRemovedVoucherAt` on the booking doc for the audit trail (mirror of `discountRejectedBy` at line 5155). Idempotent: returns success if no `voucherCode` is set (the "already removed" no-op + the "cancel cleared it" no-op compose cleanly). **(2)** new router block in `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` that calls `authenticateStaff` before invoking the handler (mirrors the `apply-discount` route shape). **(3)** new test file `guest-app/tests/api/vou-02-remove-voucher-endpoint.test.ts` (11 tests, 7 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions) — pins the contract shape (handler declaration, idempotency check, reads-before-writes shape, rateBreakdown rebuild via `calculateDiscountChain` with `voucherAmount: 0`, the `usageCount - 1` clamp shape, staff-authenticated route wiring, the booking-doc write shape that clears `voucherCode` + `voucherDiscount` + sets `totalPrice`). The 4 runtime assertions cover the row-builder math: decrement clamp at 0, the chain rebuild that widens the member-discount base, the idempotent no-op when no voucherCode is set, and the confirm/cancel/remove composition (cancel restores only what confirm decremented). **Scope decision:** this is staff-only — the guest-facing "Remove" link in `VOUCHERS.md:44` implies a separate future endpoint (the bookingId + guestEmail + token auth pattern, mirroring `/api/bookings/lookup`). The staff endpoint closes the immediate operational gap (mistaken-apply corrections); a guest endpoint would compose with this one (idempotency holds). **Touch:** `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~165 lines new handler), `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` (~15 lines new route), new test file. All 1575 guest-app tests pass (was 1564 — +11 new = 1575 — 1 unrelated pre-existing walkin BF-02 failure); 1259 admin tests pass; 553 shared tests pass; TypeScript clean.

- ✅ **VOU-03 — Voucher `applicableRoomTypes` all-or-none confirmed (closed-no-change per owner decision 2026-08-15, option (a))** (closed 2026-08-15 on `dev` @ this commit, doc-only — no code change). Per the spec at `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md:54-57` (now line 58 with the new clarifying bullet): a voucher restricted to a subset of room types applies to a booking IF AND ONLY IF every selected room type is in the subset. Both `handleCreateBooking` (line 2515-2516) and `handleCreateWalkin` (line 4301-4302) enforce this — `allSelectedTypesApplicable` must be true. The audit caught the UX gap: a mixed-type reservation cannot use a subset-restricted voucher. **Owner decision 2026-08-15: keep current behavior (option a) — voucher applies to whole booking or not at all.** This shape is consistent with the corporate-code handling and matches industry-standard "member rate" semantics (Marriott/Hilton). The spec is now explicit about the all-or-none contract (added bullet 58 in `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md`) so future audits do not re-flag this as doc drift. No code change shipped. **Touch:** `plan/features/VOUCHERS.md` (1 added bullet).

- ✅ **DSC-04 — `handleApplyReservationDiscount` atomic endpoint shipped (off-roadmap future work — owner decision option (a))** (shipped 2026-08-15 on `dev` @ `6cb75e9`, v0.268.0 → v0.269.0, found during the discount-flow review that followed VOU-02). **REAL PARTIAL-FAILURE UX BUG, CLOSED.** The admin client's reservation-scope apply used to loop `for (const targetId of targetIds) { fetch(.../api/bookings/apply-discount ... bookingId: targetId) }` over each room — when room N failed after rooms 1..N-1 succeeded, the reservation was left half-discounted and required manual correction. **Fix — `feat/apply-reservation-discount` branch off `dev`:** **(1)** new `handleApplyReservationDiscount` in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~250 lines) — accepts `{ reservationId, discountType?, voucherCode? }`, pre-transaction query for child ID discovery (`bookings.where("reservationId", "==", reservationId).get()`), then ONE `runTransaction` that re-reads every child with `transaction.get` (FOL-03 reads-before-writes), filters for eligibility (status + no-existing-discount), validates the voucher cap once against the SUM (`usageCount + eligibleChildrenCount <= usageCap`), writes the discount to every child via the same per-child math as `handleApplyBookingDiscount` (lines 4990-5064 mirror), increments `vouchers.usageCount` by `eligible.length` (single write — VOU-01 per-child semantics preserved), and stamps the standard audit-trail fields (`discountVerifiedBy`, `voucherCode`, etc.) on each child. Throws on any failure — the entire transaction aborts, no partial state. Response shape: `{ appliedTo: [...bookingIds], skipped: [...bookingIds], voucherUsageCount? }`. Idempotent: a retry with the same discount/voucher is a no-op (all eligible children already have the discount set, they go into `skipped`, cap is not re-incremented). **(2)** new router block in `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` with the same `authenticateStaff` gate as `apply-discount` (~15 lines). **(3)** admin client update — `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx:7874-7918` now uses a single conditional fetch that picks `apply-reservation-discount` when `isReservationScope` is true (scope === "reservation" && reservation context exists), falls back to `apply-discount` for single-room (byte-equivalent to a 1-child reservation). The per-targetId loop is removed; `targetIds.length` references in toast messages replaced with `selectedReservationContext.roomCount`. **(4)** MRB-12-05 source-text pin in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts` updated to pin the new atomic-shape (`isReservationScope` + `endpointPath` ternary + `apply-reservation-discount` URL) with an anti-regression assertion that the per-targetId loop is GONE. **(5)** new test file `guest-app/tests/api/dsc-04-apply-reservation-discount.test.ts` (17 tests: 11 source-text guards + 6 runtime assertions) — covers N=3 with 2 eligible (1 already discounted, `skipped` list correct), transaction rollback on mid-transaction throw (no writes land), voucher cap exceeded by N children (99 + 2 > 100 cap rejects cleanly), N=1 byte-equivalence to the existing endpoint, missing reservationId (400), missing discountType+voucherCode (400). **Touch:** `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~250 lines), `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` (~15 lines), `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx` (~40 lines refactored), `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts` (~40 lines updated), new test file. All 1593 guest-app tests pass (was 1576 — +17 DSC-04 tests); 1260 admin tests pass; 553 shared tests pass; TypeScript clean; API rebuilt (10.9mb).

- ✅ **RPT-06 — Reports `payments` collectionGroup handler resolves reservation-scope payments to per-room `bookingId` (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/reports-payments-show-booking-ref` @ `93b6d1e`, v0.273.0 → v0.273.2, found during the 2026-08-19 staging audit when staff reported GUIDs in the Daily Close Transactions Ledger's "Booking" column). **REAL BUG, STAFF-FACING UX.** Sibling of RPT-04 (which patched the `refunds` collectionGroup with the `isReservationRefund` + `data.bookingId` pattern) and RPT-05 (which patched the Full Backup XLSX payments loop). The Reports page's `payments` collectionGroup subscription at `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx:306-356` was the third consumer of the same dual-write shape (post-MRB-01 payments live at `reservations/{id}/payments/{id}`, legacy at `bookings/{id}/payments/{id}` per MRB-04 Phase 2.x / decision #159) and was the only one still missing the routing. The `parentDocumentId` fallback used the path's parent — for a reservation-scope payment that resolves to the `reservationId` (a Firestore auto-minted UUID), not the per-room `bookingId` the `bookingDisplayById` map keys on. The lookup missed → fell back to the raw UUID → the Daily Close ledger rendered `c749ebfa-6a50-40ac-acaf-d282dec0296e` instead of the human-readable `SI-20260819-00001` (the `Booking.bookingRef`). The `GUEST / ROOM` column was empty for the same reason. **Fix — mirror the `refunds` listener's pattern:** added `isReservationPayment = !isStoreTender && paymentDoc.ref.path.startsWith("reservations/")` and routed the `bookingId` through `data.bookingId || parentDocumentId` (the per-room attribution stamp written at `bookings.ts:7647`). The display map lookup now resolves every row that has a per-room attribution. New test file `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-06-payments-show-booking-ref.test.ts` (4 source-text guards: path detection, `data.bookingId` fallback, legacy `parentDocumentId` else branch, store-tender no-regression). FL-05 test relaxed the over-strict ternary-shape regex to the discriminator + store-tender branch (the test's actual intent; the non-store branch is allowed to vary for the reservation routing). MD: `plan/features/REPORTS.md §RPT-06` captured. **Touch:** `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx:306-356` (listener only — no other file changed), `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-06-payments-show-booking-ref.test.ts` (new), `admin-app/src/__tests__/finance-lifecycle-fl05.test.ts` (relaxed shape regex), `plan/features/REPORTS.md` (RPT-06 entry in UX checklist).

- ✅ **RPT-07 — Reports `payments` join renders `reservationRef` + `leadGuestName` for reservation-level payments (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/reports-payments-show-booking-ref` @ `9f39bdb`, v0.273.2 → v0.273.3, owner signoff 2026-08-19 to build immediately rather than observe). **Residual UX gap from RPT-06, closed.** The RPT-06 fix routed reservation-scope payments through `data.bookingId || parentDocumentId`, but `data.bookingId` is `null` for reservation-level payments (per the MRB-04 Phase 2.x attribution contract at `bookings.ts:7638-7640` — *"per-room attribution — `null` for reservation-level payments; non-null when the staff ties a payment to a specific room's add-on"*). The RPT-06 fallback to `parentDocumentId` (the `reservationId`) would still render the raw UUID for those rows. **Fix — second-tier reservation lookup in the `payments` join:** the Reports page now consults a `reservationMetaById` map (built from AdminContext's `reservations` slice — the same `subscribeToReservations` listener MRB-12 added) as a fallback when the per-room `bookingDisplayById.get(bookingId)` lookup misses. The chain renders the public `R-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN` `reservationRef` in the "Booking" column + the `leadGuestName` (per MRB-02 contract) in the "GUEST / ROOM" column. The `!display ? reservationMetaById.get(bookingId) : null` guard prevents a per-room bookingId from being shadowed by the reservation map (defensive — the two id shapes differ: bookings use Firestore auto-id 20 chars, reservations use RFC4122 UUID 36 chars, so a collision is impossible in practice). The final fallback to `payment.bookingId` (the raw reservationId UUID) is preserved for any future write path that drops the reservation header — same defensive shape RPT-06 had pre-fix. New test file `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-07-reservation-level-payment-display.test.ts` (6 source-text guards: context destructure, map builder, `R-` ref fallback, `leadGuestName` fallback, `!display` precedence guard, deps array). RPT-04's `rpt-04-refunds-collectiongroup-merge.test.ts` dep pin relaxed from `[rawPayments, rawRefunds, bookingDisplayById]` to `bookingDisplayById[^\]]*\]` to allow this addition without a re-pin. MD: `plan/features/REPORTS.md §RPT-07` captured. **Touch:** `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (added `reservations` to the AdminContext destructure + new `reservationMetaById` memo + 3rd branch in the `payments` join), `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-07-reservation-level-payment-display.test.ts` (new), `admin-app/src/__tests__/rpt-04-refunds-collectiongroup-merge.test.ts` (relaxed dep pin regex), `plan/features/REPORTS.md` (RPT-07 entry in UX checklist). **No new collectionGroup read** — the AdminContext's existing `subscribeToReservations` listener is the canonical source. Owner decision 2026-08-19: build immediately rather than wait for an observed row (per the queued entry's explicit "address at the same time as the next Reports audit pass or when a real row surfaces" — owner chose the audit-pass option).

## Audit Session — 2026-08-14 (RPT-05 → DSC-04)

A single-day audit + fix pass across 7 Spark Inn feature surfaces. Found 2 real bugs, 1 missing handler, 1 doc-drift, 1 test-discipline gap, and 2 doc-drift items needing owner decision. The bug class — **per-child counter drift** — recurred in 2 of the 7 surfaces; the test-discipline retro (8 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions per fix) was the key to catching it.

### Per-fix summary (chronological)

| # | Entry | Branch | Feature commit | Severity | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **RPT-05** — Reports Full Backup XLSX missing reservation-scope payments + charges | `fix/rpt-05-full-backup-reservation-payments` | `245837c` | Real bug | Per-child collectionGroup drift |
| 2 | **EXB-12.1** — `booking-room-cart.test.ts` fixture missing `extraBedBreakfast` field | `fix/exb-12-booking-room-cart-fixture-mismatch` | `7453c7f` | Test bug (pre-existing on dev since EXB-12) | Test discipline |
| 3 | **VOU-01** — Voucher `usageCount` increment was per-reservation, spec requires per-child | `fix/voucher-usagecount-per-child` | `15cd1e8` | Real bug (revenue impact — cap off by N for multi-room) | Per-child counter drift |
| 4 | **STR-01** — `handleConfirmStoreOrder` handler missing (spec promised, never built) | `feat/store-confirm-stock-decrement` | `d462c8a` | Real bug (inventory drift — stock never decremented) | Missing feature |
| 5 | **VOU-02** — `handleRemoveVoucher` endpoint missing (spec promised, never built) | `feat/voucher-remove-endpoint` | `fa2d1be` | Doc drift (no staff voucher-removal path; cancel was the only option) | Missing feature |
| 6 | **VOU-03** — Voucher `applicableRoomTypes` all-or-none vs per-room — owner decision (a): keep current | `docs/vou-03-closed-no-change` | this commit | ✅ closed-no-change (doc-only) | UX gap, no code |
| 7 | **DSC-04** — `applyReservationDiscount` atomic endpoint — owner decision (a): full atomic server endpoint | `feat/apply-reservation-discount` | `d7d5278` | ✅ shipped (real partial-failure UX bug, closed) | Atomicity |

Plus the test-discipline gap closed inline: **discount-scope loop pin** at `ab2d215` (5-line source-text regex guard on `BookingsPage.tsx:7874-7898` — pinned the `for (const targetId of targetIds) { ... fetch(.../api/bookings/apply-discount ... bookingId: targetId ...) }` loop body so a future refactor that collapses the loop silently miscounting `vouchers.usageCount` is caught at the source level). **Updated 2026-08-15 in DSC-04 commit `d7d5278`:** the loop is removed in favor of the atomic `apply-reservation-discount` endpoint; the MRB-12-05 pin test now asserts the new atomic shape AND anti-regresses that the loop is GONE.

### Bug class — what we observed

The recurring shape: **a spec change added per-child / multi-room aggregate semantics, but a downstream consumer that iterates over the new shape didn't get updated.** The fix is mechanical — increment by `N` not by `1` — but the **detection** is what's hard: regex tests on the simple case pass while runtime drift hides. The v0.264.9 retrofit pattern (1 runtime assertion per regression-guard test file) was the unlock. The pre-VOU-01 source-text pin `+ 1` matched the regex — the runtime assertion reproducing the row-builder math against a 3-room fixture caught the actual bug.

### What we audited + didn't fix (clean surfaces)

- **Spark Rewards loyalty** — `earn-${bookingId}` / `clawback-${bookingId}` pairing intact (MRB-15-07). Per-child semantics correct.
- **Spark Essentials Store** — per-order, not per-child aggregation; clean for this bug class. Separate missing-handler bug (`handleConfirmStoreOrder`) was fixed as STR-01.
- **Intercom Inbox** — `intercoms/{roomId}/messages` is room-scoped; no aggregation across rooms.
- **Notification Center** — one `notifications` doc per event; intentionally per-reservation, not per-child.
- **Discount flow (apply-discount + reject-discount)** — per-call semantics, client orchestrates reservation-scope loop. Correct + now has test pin.
- **Corporate Booking** — per-room at create (`+ assignedRooms.length`), `Map<code, count>` at reservation-scope cancel, `- 1` at room-scope cancel. Spec-accurate.
- **House Rules emails** — global per-property string; no per-child fan-out.
- **DASHBOARD-OVERVIEW** — per-booking aggregated view.

### Test discipline retrofitted

- **VOU-01** test file: 10 tests (6 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions). Pattern: source-text pins the new variable name at the function-declaration site (`childrenWithVoucherCount`), runtime reproduces the row-builder against a 3-room fixture.
- **STR-01** test file: 12 tests (8 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions). Pattern: pins handler declaration + reads-before-writes shape + decrement math + idempotency + OUT_OF_STOCK guard + router auth wiring.
- **VOU-02** test file: 11 tests (7 source-text guards + 4 runtime assertions). Pattern: mirrors VOU-01's per-child counter pattern + rateBreakdown rebuild via `calculateDiscountChain` + audit-trail fields.
- **discount-scope loop pin** test: 1 test, single source-text regex on `BookingsPage.tsx:7874-7898`. Pin was cheap because the source text is small.

- **DSC-04** (`applyReservationDiscount` atomic endpoint): **owner decision 2026-08-15: option (a) — full atomic server endpoint.** Shipped on `dev` @ `6cb75e9` (v0.268.0 → v0.269.0) as `handleApplyReservationDiscount` in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts:5077+` + new `/api/bookings/apply-reservation-discount` route + admin client refactor. ### Future audit pass suggestions

- **Intercom inbox + notification fan-out** for any future per-child semantics — the current per-room + per-event shapes are correct, but any new aggregate would need the same audit treatment.
- **Spec hygiene** — every feature spec that mentions a handler name should be auditable against the actual exports in the corresponding handlers file. STR-01 + VOU-02 are the second + third "spec promised, code didn't build" findings; a one-line CI check (`grep -nE "handle\w+" plan/features/*.md | grep -v "^plan/features/.*:[0-9]*:#"`) would catch future drift.

Future audit passes can reuse the pattern.

### Finance scope boundaries & recommendations
- ⏸ Expenses & P&L tracking — out of scope; system is a PMS, not accounting software; exports feed external bookkeeping/BIR
- ⏸ Day-locking / night-audit snapshots — deferred at 14-room scale; payments are already append-only at the rules level, which covers the cash side; revisit if historical figures drift or staff grows
- ⏸ **FLR-03 — Bound Reports ledger listeners** *(deferred with trigger)* — `collectionGroup("payments"/"charges")` loads full ledger history live on every Reports visit; fine at 14 rooms, linear forever on Blaze. **Trigger: revisit at ~1 year of operation** — switch to `recordedAt`-bounded queries; all-time Receivables fall back to one-shot `getDocs`.
- 🔄 **FLR-05 — Operational handover** *(owner-facing)* — handover in `FINANCE-LIFECYCLE-HANDOVER-2026-07-14.md`. Daily Close convention + accountant VAT review + staging money-path walkthrough have explicit checklists/evidence. **Remaining:** accountant confirmation + owner sign-off before next `dev → main` milestone.

### Booking Architecture Refactor (BAR) — deferred (target: post-MRB-15)

> Spec: [`plan/features/BOOKING-UNIFIED.md`](plan/features/BOOKING-UNIFIED.md). Decision record: `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #202` (filed 2026-08-08). Visual tour: `plan/project/BAR-01-booking-flow-tour.html` (11-step post-refactor walkthrough with partial-payment scenario).
>
> **Context.** The post-MRB-01 `reservations/{id}` doc has grown into a full first-class entity across 16 separate steps (MRB-01..15 + CRL-07/08 + FOL-03/05) — payments subcollection, refunds subcollection, denormalized `roomCount` / `activeRoomCount` / `cancelledRoomCount` / `checkedInRoomCount` / `checkedOutRoomCount` counter fields, derived `paymentStatus` aggregate mirror. FOL-03 + FOL-05 are surface-level examples of the cost: every payment / checkin / checkout handler now needs a sibling-flip pass + reads-before-writes ordering. The refactor: **1 booking, 1 lifecycle, 1 payment path, N rooms in a `rooms: BookingRoomLine[]` array on the booking.**
>
> **Trigger conditions (all 3 must be true to start):**
> 1. MRB-15 has fully shipped (the transactional counters work is the natural milestone — current state ✅).
> 2. The team has bandwidth for a 6-month project (solo-dev shop) / 2-month project (3-engineer team). No P0 work in flight.
> 3. The next "multi-room" feature (apply-discount whole-reservation, partial refund, per-room check-in, per-room add-on) is on the roadmap — refactor first, then the new feature ships on the new shape.
> 4. **(Added by the 2026-08-08 audit) Availability has moved off per-room-doc conflict checks.** The double-booking transaction queries `bookings where roomId == X and status in ROOM_OCCUPYING_STATUSES` (`guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts`, plus 8 more `where("roomId", …)` call sites in room-blocks + corporate-inquiries), then filters date overlap in memory. Collapsing the children into `rooms[]` removes the queryable per-room row: `roomId` needs a denormalized `roomIds: string[]` for `array-contains`, per-room `status` can no longer be filtered server-side, and per-room date divergence (MRB-14 `actualDateRange`) can't be indexed either. **BAR-01 makes the system's most safety-critical transaction harder, not easier.** It only becomes the right shape if availability moves to room-type inventory (free units per type per night) instead of per-room conflict checks — a separate project, not part of BAR-01.
>
> **Net code change: ~-1,200 lines** (FOL-05's ~200 lines of sibling-flip + MRB-15-03's counter-proliferation + MRB-13's reservation-scope cancel + MRB-14's add-room endpoint + CRL-07's dual-source liability read all deleted; the new `rooms[]` array plumbing is ~300-500 lines). **Line count is not a business outcome** — it is not on its own a reason to spend a 6-month solo project with an irreversible step on a live money system.
>
> **Audit verdict (2026-08-08):** the diagnosis in #202 is sound, the destination shape is not. Do **BAR-02 + BAR-03** below first — they capture most of the day-to-day relief with no data migration, no rules changes, and no irreversible step. Revisit BAR-01 only if the pain persists after those ship *and* trigger condition 4 is met.

#### What gets deleted (the simplification)

After step 6, the codebase loses ~1,500-2,000 lines and gains ~300-500. Net **~-1,200 lines**.

- **FOL-05 sibling-flip pass** — ~200 lines of `siblingChildBookings` pre-read + `postUpdateChildStatuses` array + per-child `transaction.update` calls across 3 handlers. Gone.
- **FOL-03 reads-before-writes ordering** — still good practice, but no longer load-bearing for payment handlers. Check-in / check-out handlers still need it.
- **MRB-15-03 transactional counters** — ~5 denormalized counter fields (`roomCount` / `activeRoomCount` / `cancelledRoomCount` / `checkedInRoomCount` / `checkedOutRoomCount`) + the `computeReservationAggregatePaymentStatus` helper usage. Gone. Counters are derived on-read.
- **MRB-13 reservation-scope cancel** — the `Map<code, count>` dedup + the `cancelledRoomCount` increment + the `paymentStatus` aggregate mirror. Gone. Just "cancel the booking, mutate `rooms[]`."
- **MRB-14 add-room** — the new endpoint + the separate room-line derivation. Gone. Just "push a new entry into `rooms[]`."
- **CRL-07 liability snapshot** — the dual-source read (reservation header for N>1, booking doc for legacy). Gone. Always on the booking now.
- **The `reservations` collection** — empty. The inline `onSnapshot(collection(db, "reservations"))` effect in `AdminContext.tsx` + the `getDocs` in `LiabilityTab.tsx` are removed. `collectionGroup("payments")` aggregate is removed.
- **The `reservations` security-rules block** — the top-level match + its `payments` / `refunds` / `charges` children, plus any reservation-parented index.
- **The folio dual-source branches** — every `reservationId ? reservations/… : bookings/…` ternary in `BookingsPage.tsx` collapses to the booking path.

#### What this enables (the next round of features)

Once the booking is one doc with one lifecycle, the next round of features is much smaller. All are "mutate `rooms[i]`, no N-doc iteration, no header mirror":

- Apply discount to whole reservation (one doc write, no scope picker).
- Partial refund (one entry into refunds subcollection with per-room allocation; `rooms[].paidAmount` updates in the same transaction).
- Per-room check-in (one guest checks in, others don't yet — `rooms[i].status` flip is local, no child-doc write).
- Multi-night add-on per room (push into `rooms[i].addOns: []`).
- Cancellation policy per room (`rooms[i].cancellationPolicySnapshot` is local, no `cancellableChildren` set derivation).

#### Open questions for the team (resolve at step 0)
