- **Wire ref contract.** Today booking has `bookingRef` (`SI-XXXXX`) AND `reservationRef` (`R-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN`). Post-refactor: rename `bookingRef` → `reservationRef` everywhere (cleaner, bigger blast radius) or keep both (back-compat)?
- **Counter derivation on-read vs small denormalized snapshot.** N=1-3 (current ceiling) is fine on-read. N=10+ rooms: consider a 2-field denormalized snapshot (`cancelledRoomCount`, `activeRoomCount`) computed transactionally. Not a problem today.
- **`rooms` array size cap.** 14-room property with one guest booking all 14 rooms = `rooms.length === 14`. Well under 1 MiB. For 100+ rooms (white-label), consider `rooms` subcollection when `rooms.length > 20`. Defer to that future.
- **`roomId` + `roomNumber` + `roomType` denormalization staleness.** Same drift risk that exists today. Not a new problem.

#### Related entries to be marked "superseded by #202" when the migration completes

- FOL-01 (the `isPaymentVerified` helper is unchanged but the FOL-05 sibling-flip contract it depends on is gone)
- FOL-03 (reads-before-writes is no longer load-bearing for payment handlers)
- FOL-05 (the sibling-flip pass is deleted)
- MRB-01 (the wrapper doc is gone)
- MRB-05 (the N>1 aggregate is gone, computed on-read)
- MRB-13 (the reservation-scope cancel is "cancel the booking")
- MRB-14 (add-room is "push a room")
- MRB-15-03 (the counter-proliferation is gone)
- CRL-07 (the dual-source liability read is gone; always on the booking)

#### Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in BAR-01)

- Subcollection-vs-array choice for the `rooms` field when N>20 (defer to white-label era when individual bookings might hit 50+ rooms).
- Per-room age-tier pricing (CHD-13 mirrors the `/book` shape on the homepage; deeper per-tier pricing is a separate project).
- Per-room discount types (today's discount scope is reservation-level via MRB-12's segmented control; per-room discounts would extend the discount system, not the booking shape).

- ✅ **CHD-11.3 — Symmetric auto-bump + remove all per-type caps from the picker (off-roadmap UX refinement)** (shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump`, per decision #194; v0.261.0 → v0.262.0). Operator-reported 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.2 review): "the maximum I can have is still 3 — the max config I can have is 4 adults and 3 children, when I make it 6 guests, no more children it becomes 0. Can we not have any guards to the numbers?" Two residual UX issues that violate the CHD-11 "exploration-first, validation-on-commit" promise. **Issue 1: "the maximum I can have is still 3"** — the per-type cap (`selectedMaxSelectableChildren`) is still firing in `setOccupancy` and `updateChildren`. Even with CHD-11.2 (which removed the per-type cap from the `+` button's `disabled` condition), the value silently snaps back to `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` when the user clicks `+` past the cap. **Issue 2: "when I make it 6 guests, no more children it becomes 0"** — the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp in `setOccupancy` fires when the user lowers `guests` below `children + 1`. **Root cause** — the client-side guards in `setOccupancy` + `updateChildren` are enforcing things the submit gate can validate. **Fix — three-part change to `BookingPage.tsx`.** **(1) Remove the per-type cap (`selectedMaxSelectableChildren`) from `setOccupancy` + `updateChildren`.** The `safeChildren` clamp chain becomes `Math.max(0, Math.min(nextChildren, safeGuests - 1))` (the "at least 1 adult" invariant only). The `updateChildren`'s `desiredChildren` becomes `Math.max(nextChildren, 0)`. The `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (used by the chip's hint text). **(2) Add a symmetric auto-bump to `updateGuests`.** If the user lowers `guests` to a value where `children + 1 > guests`, auto-bump `guests` up to `children + 1`. The new `updateGuests` computes `newGuests = Math.max(nextGuests, numChildren + 1)` and calls `setOccupancy(newGuests, numChildren)`. The "at least 1 adult" invariant is maintained via auto-bump, not clamp. **(3) Keep the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp in `setOccupancy` as defense in depth.** The server-side CHD-05 validation is the authoritative gate. **What the user sees after the change** — picker is now a pure exploration surface. Click `+` to 4 children with guests=2 → auto-bumps to guests=5, children=4. The "Fits your group" chip says "Doesn't fit" (depending on the room's capacity). Set guests=1 with children=3 → auto-bumps guests to 4, children=3 (preserved). No more silent snap-backs. No more "children becomes 0" surprises. **Why the symmetric auto-bump is the right shape** — the pre-CHD-11.3 model was asymmetric (only `updateChildren` auto-bumped, `updateGuests` clamped). The asymmetry is unintuitive. The user expects "I pick a number, the system figures out the right total." The fix is to make `updateGuests` symmetric: both functions bump `guests` up; neither clamps `children` down. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `guests` state stays, `numChildren` state stays, `numAdults = max(0, guests - numChildren)` derivation stays, `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (used by the chip's hint text), `setOccupancy` helper stays, `updateChildren` function stays, `updateGuests` function stays. The clamping chain in `setOccupancy` is updated (per-type cap removed). **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~10 lines changed: 3 lines in `setOccupancy`, 3 lines in `updateChildren`, 3 lines in `updateGuests`, plus comment blocks). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump.test.ts` (~11 source-text guards). Update 2 existing tests in `chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts` and `chd-05-guest-child-cap.test.ts` to remove the `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` references. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.3 — Symmetric Auto-Bump + Remove All Per-Type Caps from the Picker`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #194`. **Rejected** — keep the per-type cap in `setOccupancy` but raise it to a higher value (per-type cap is a soft constraint, not a domain rule); remove the per-type cap but keep the asymmetric auto-bump (asymmetry is unintuitive); remove the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp entirely (deep-links and race conditions could bypass it); show a warning when the user picks more children than the room supports (chip + submit gate are the existing surfaces); auto-add a second room when the user is over the cap (presumptuous). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server still validates; the client-side auto-bump + clamp mirror this; the server is the authoritative gate), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the chip + submit gate are the commit surfaces; the CHD-11 promise is now fully realized), CHD-11.1 (the auto-bump in `updateChildren` is unchanged; CHD-11.3 adds a symmetric auto-bump in `updateGuests`), CHD-11.2 (the picker cap is unchanged; the per-type cap is now gone from the picker), CHD-12 (unchanged), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (unchanged), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (unrelated), CHD-13 (unchanged), EXB-11.3 (independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.3)** — per-room-type picker cap; a "Room full" warning separate from the chip; a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; visual feedback when the auto-bump fires; server-side validation for the per-type cap; per-type cap on the homepage widget.

- ✅ **CHD-11.4 — Free expression in the picker (submit-gate enforces "at least 1 adult") (off-roadmap UX refinement)** (shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-4-picker-free-expression`, per decision #195; v0.262.0 → v0.263.0). Operator-reported 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.3 review): "but why is it that there is a gate or connection to the number of adults and children? I want the user to freely express how many people they are in all (adults and children)." Even with the per-type caps gone (CHD-11.3), the **"at least 1 adult" invariant** is still auto-bumping the Guests stepper when the user adds children — e.g., with 2 guests, clicking `+` on children silently bumps Guests from 2 to 3 to maintain 1 adult. The user wants the picker to be a free expression surface with no auto-bump; the "at least 1 adult" rule is enforced via the submit gate (Continue button disabled when `numAdults < 1`). **Root cause** — the CHD-11.1 auto-bump in `updateChildren` (`newGuests = max(guests, desiredChildren + 1)`) + the CHD-11.3 symmetric auto-bump in `updateGuests` (`newGuests = max(nextGuests, numChildren + 1)`) + the CHD-11.3 defense-in-depth clamp in `setOccupancy` (`Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` floor) are enforcing "at least 1 adult" via auto-bump + clamp instead of via the submit gate. **Fix — three-part change to `BookingPage.tsx` reversing CHD-11.1 + CHD-11.3.** **(1) Remove the auto-bump in `updateChildren`.** The new `updateChildren` calls `setOccupancy(guests, desiredChildren)` directly (no `newGuests = max(guests, desiredChildren + 1)` intermediate). **(2) Remove the symmetric auto-bump in `updateGuests` (reverses CHD-11.3).** The new `updateGuests` calls `setOccupancy(nextGuests, numChildren)` directly (no `newGuests = max(nextGuests, numChildren + 1)` intermediate). **(3) Remove the `Math.max(0, safeGuests - 1)` clamp in `setOccupancy`'s `safeChildren` chain (reverses CHD-11.3's defense-in-depth).** The new chain is `Math.max(0, Math.min(nextChildren, safeGuests))` — children can be up to `safeGuests`, so `numAdults = safeGuests - safeChildren` can be 0. **What the user sees after the change** — picker is a free expression surface. Set Guests=5, Children=5 → picker shows 5 / 5 / 0 adults. The Continue button is disabled with "Choose enough rooms to assign every adult and child, with at least one adult in each room." The user adds an adult (or removes a child) and Continue re-enables. Lower Guests from 3 to 1 with Children=3 → no clamp, Children stays at 3, numAdults = max(0, 1-3) = 0, submit disabled. **Why the submit gate is the right enforcement surface** — the "at least 1 adult" rule is a domain constraint (per CHD-05, the server requires at least 1 guest per room). The submit gate is the existing surface for domain constraints. The auto-bump was a UX shortcut that **prevented the user from exploring the invalid state** — the user benefits from being able to see the state and being told by the submit gate why it's invalid. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `guests` state stays, `numChildren` state stays, `numAdults = max(0, guests - numChildren)` derivation stays (line 221), `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation stays (CHD-11.3), `setOccupancy` helper stays (clamp chain updated), `updateChildren` function stays (auto-bump removed), `updateGuests` function stays (symmetric auto-bump removed). Cart URL serialization (`?guests=N&children=N`) stays. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~6 lines changed: 1 line in `setOccupancy`, 2 lines in `updateChildren`, 2 lines in `updateGuests`, plus comment blocks). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-4-picker-free-expression.test.ts` (~10 source-text guards). Update 3 prior tests in `chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts`, `chd-11-2-picker-cap-soft-10.test.ts`, `chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump.test.ts` to reflect the removed auto-bumps. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.4 — Free Expression in the Picker (Submit-Gate Enforces "At Least 1 Adult")`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #195`. **Rejected** — remove the "at least 1 adult" rule entirely (relax the server contract; the per-room "at least 1 guest" is a real domain rule per CHD-05); add a separate "Adults" stepper (more UI; the current "Guests" + "Children" pair with `numAdults = guests - children` derived is sufficient); keep the auto-bump but add a "You can have 0 adults" toggle (more UI than a free-expression picker); auto-bump with a toast notification (surfaces the auto-bump but doesn't give the user the freedom to explore). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server contract unchanged; the client's submit gate mirrors the per-room "at least 1 guest" check), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the chip + submit gate are the only validation surfaces; the CHD-11 promise is now fully realized), CHD-11.1 (auto-bump in `updateChildren` is reversed by CHD-11.4), CHD-11.2 (soft cap of 10 stays), CHD-11.3 (the symmetric auto-bump is reversed by CHD-11.4; the per-type cap removal stays), CHD-12 (cart summary unchanged), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (extras toggle re-fires when `numAdults` or `numChildren` change), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (unrelated), CHD-13 (homepage widget already has separate Adults + Children steppers; the /book picker's free expression is a separate concern), EXB-11.3 (independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.4)** — add a separate "Adults" stepper to the /book picker; a "0 adults + N children" preset button; visual feedback when the submit button is disabled due to 0 adults; server-side allow `numAdults: 0` per-room (relax CHD-05); a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action.

- ✅ **Homepage guests popover stepper overflow (off-roadmap UI fix)** (shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/guests-popover-overflow`, no decision; v0.264.5 → v0.264.6). Operator-reported 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-12 review, with screenshot): the homepage "Guests" popover (per CHD-13) opened to show what looked like a duplicate set of Adults / Children `−` `+` controls hanging to the right of the white popover card. **Root cause** — the popover at `HomePage.tsx:311` used `absolute left-0 right-0 top-full`, which pinned its width to the parent trigger's width (~144px on `lg` via the surrounding `lg:min-w-36`). The popover's content (the "Children (0–11)" label + three 44px stepper buttons + count) needs ~290px to fit, so the stepper buttons overflowed the white card to the right and looked like a second standalone stepper widget. The behavior was correct (single state, single popover) — the visual was just clipped. **Fix — one-line className change at `HomePage.tsx:311`.** Replace `absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-30 mt-2` with `absolute left-0 top-full z-30 mt-2 w-72 … sm:w-80`. Drop `right-0` (the explicit width handles the sizing) and add `w-72` (288px) on mobile / `sm:w-80` (320px) on `sm+` so the popover is left-aligned to the trigger and wide enough to hold both stepper rows + the "Done" button inside the white card. The click-outside / Escape dismissal, the `aria-haspopup` / `role="dialog"` / `aria-labelledby` contract, and the `setPopoverOpen` state are all unchanged. The `adults` / `children` state and the `setAdults` / `setChildren` handlers are unchanged. **What the user sees after the change** — opens the "Guests" popover: a single white card with the "Adults" row (label + `−` `2` `+`), the "Children (0–11)" row (label + `−` `0` `+`), and the "Done" button. No duplicate controls to the right. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/HomePage.tsx` (1 line, 1 file). **Tests** — none added (visual overflow with no behavior change; existing CHD-13 source-text guards stay green). **MDs** — this entry only (no new spec, no new decision — a one-line CSS width tweak doesn't warrant a `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md` section or a `DECISIONS-FEATURES.md` entry). **Rejected** — keep `right-0` and add `min-w-72` (with `right-0` still set, the popover would overflow to the LEFT of the trigger because `min-width` overrides the `right` anchor; ugly and counter-intuitive); use `w-max` or no width (the popover would size to content but the right edge would be unconstrained, potentially clipping off the viewport on narrow screens); widen the trigger instead (the trigger's width is content-driven by the "N adults, M children" label; making the trigger wider doesn't help the popover); reduce the stepper button size (44px is the touch-target minimum per the design system; shrinking it regresses mobile UX); move the stepper rows to stack label-above-buttons (more vertical space, changes the visual rhythm vs. the `/book` picker for no real win). **Gates** — CHD-13 (the popover's behavior, state, and aria contract are unchanged; only the visual width changes), EXB-12 (unrelated; extra-bed breakfast toggle on `/book`), CHD-11.5 (the homepage widget already had separate Adults + Children steppers; the URL contract is unchanged). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in this fix)** — a `useResizeObserver` on the popover to dynamically size based on the trigger's content; a max-width on the popover to prevent it from exceeding the viewport on very wide triggers; align the popover to the right edge of the trigger on mobile (so it doesn't push off-screen).

- ✅ **EXB-12 strict-schema regression (off-roadmap hotfix)** (shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/exb-12-strict-schema-regression`, no new decision; v0.264.6 → v0.264.7). Operator-reported 2026-08-06 (post-EXB-12 review): every `/book` POST returns `400 — "Please check the booking details — a required field is missing or invalid."` even when all fields are filled. Console shows `Confirm booking error: Error: Please check the booking details — a required field is missing or invalid.`. The booking UI completes Steps 1–4, the client POSTs to `/api/bookings/create`, and the server's strict Zod schema rejects the body on the first unrecognized key. **Root cause** — the EXB-12 commit (v0.264.5, `cfb56a6`) added the new `extraBedBreakfast` field to the **client** body (top-level + per `roomSelections[i]`, defaulting to `false` so the field is always present) and to the server's internal logic (`validatedRoomStays` invariant enforcement, pricing loop, booking-doc snapshot, rate-breakdown rebuild) — but **forgot to declare the field in the two strict Zod schemas that gate the public POST**: `createBookingSchema` (the top-level body, `bookings.ts:1121`) and `publicRoomSelectionSchema` (the per-room shape, `bookings.ts:1069`). Both schemas are `.strict()`, so any unknown key fails the parse at the very top of `handleCreateBooking` and the handler returns the generic 400. The pre-EXB-12 server's `BookingRoomCartItem` had no `extraBedBreakfast` field, so the schemas were never updated when the client gained the field. **Why the EXB-12 test surface didn't catch it** — `guest-app/tests/api/exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast.test.ts` is 100% source-text regex (per `plan/docs/CONTRIBUTING.md §Testing` for fast deterministic guards). Its one schema-shaped assertion (line 109) regex-greps `shared/schemas/booking.ts` — a different file from the two strict server handler schemas. No test actually instantiates the schemas or POSTs through the API. So "16/16 pass" told us nothing about runtime behavior. The root cause is a missing test discipline: the test surface relied on regex rather than exercising the actual schema objects. **Fix — three-part change.** **(1) Add `extraBedBreakfast: z.boolean().optional()` to `publicRoomSelectionSchema` at `bookings.ts:1069-1080`.** Per-room, optional, the server's `validatedRoomStays` loop already enforces the invariant `extraBedBreakfast implies extraBedCount > 0` and reads the field from `selection.extraBedBreakfast` — the schema just needs to accept it. **(2) Add `extraBedBreakfast: z.boolean().optional()` to `createBookingSchema` at `bookings.ts:1121-1148`.** Top-level, optional, kept for wire back-compat with the EXB-12 client shape; the server's authoritative value lives on each `roomSelections[i].extraBedBreakfast` (per-room pricing + invariant). **(3) Export both schemas from `bookings.ts`** (`export const publicRoomSelectionSchema` + `export const createBookingSchema`) so the new test can import them. The schemas are pure Zod — no Firebase dependency at parse time — so importing them from a unit test is safe. **What the user sees after the change** — completes Steps 1–4 → POST `/api/bookings/create` returns 200 → navigates to `/book/confirm?bookingRef=...&rooms=...` with the success payload. No code change is needed on the client side — the client is already sending the correct EXB-12 body shape. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. The booking doc shape already includes `extraBedBreakfast` per `shared/types/index.ts` (added in v0.264.5). **Implementation** — `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (2 field additions + 2 export keywords, 1 file). **Tests — new `guest-app/tests/api/exb-12-strict-schema-acceptance.test.ts` (3 runtime assertions).** **(1) `createBookingSchema.safeParse` accepts the full `/book` body shape the client sends.** The test imports the actual exported schema, calls `safeParse` on a representative `/book` body, and asserts `success === true`. Pre-fix this would have failed with `unrecognized_keys: ["extraBedBreakfast"]`. **(2) `publicRoomSelectionSchema.safeParse` accepts each `roomSelections[i]`.** Same shape, per-room. **(3) `extraBedBreakfast=true` with `extraBedCount>0` is accepted** (pins the split of concerns: schema = shape, handler = business rules; the invariant lives in the handler, not the schema). The test uses `vi.mock` to stub the firebase-admin module so the handler file can be imported without a live Firebase project. No emulator, no HTTP, no JWT — pure Zod parse in <100ms. **What this guards against going forward** — the entire class of "client gains a new field, server's strict Zod schema is not updated" regressions. The next time anyone adds a field to the public `/book` body, the assertion will fail with a clear `unrecognized_keys` error and the dev will know to add the field to the schema. **MDs** — this entry only (no new spec, no new decision — the EXB-12 spec at `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §EXB-12` and decision #199 already document the field; this hotfix is a missing-step correction, not a new feature). **Rejected** — remove `.strict()` from the schemas (would silently accept typos and unknown fields; the strictness is the point — it catches contract drift at parse time, not at runtime); add a generic allow-list of unknown fields at the top level (the same as removing `.strict()` for the top-level body; same risk); keep the schemas non-strict for `extraBedBreakfast` only (special-casing defeats the point — every new field would need its own special case); revert the client change so `extraBedBreakfast` is omitted from the body when `false` (would require conditional JSON serialization, more client complexity, and the field is now part of the canonical EXB-12 wire shape — every new caller would have to know to omit it); fix only the top-level schema and tell the per-room schema to drop `extraBedBreakfast` (would require the per-room logic to not read the field, but the pricing loop at `bookings.ts:2030` already reads `selection.extraBedBreakfast` — that's the whole point of EXB-12); add the field to the schema with a tighter type (e.g., `z.literal(false)`) (the helper accepts `boolean | null`; the strict schema is the place to be loose, the handler is the place to be strict — matches the existing pattern with `breakfastIncludesChildren`); add a one-off `safeParse` regex test in the existing `exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast.test.ts` (regex tests would have caught the original bug only by accident — the regex already in that file targets `shared/schemas/booking.ts`, a different file; a new regex test for the handler file would have the same blind spot as the original test — only a real `safeParse` test catches the actual runtime behavior). **Gates** — EXB-12 (the field is now accepted end-to-end; the client + helper + handler + booking doc + rate-breakdown rebuild are unchanged), EXB-11 (the underlying extra-bed toggle contract is unchanged), EXB-11.1..5 (unchanged), CHD-10 (the adult/child split + `breakfastIncludesChildren` toggle is unchanged), MRB-06 (the `roomSelections[]` array shape is unchanged; only the per-room schema accepts the new field), MRB-11 (the revenue allocation snapshot is unchanged), BI-12 (the past-date invariant is unchanged), BI-16 (the input-validation strictness is preserved — `.strict()` stays). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in this hotfix)** — extend the runtime test to also assert the handler-level business rules (e.g., "the handler rejects `extraBedBreakfast=true` with `extraBedCount=0`" — already covered by the existing `validatedRoomStays` invariant test, but a direct handler-level test would be defense in depth); add a one-time `vitest` config check that fails if any test file under `guest-app/tests/api/` is *only* source-text regex (would force the next test to include at least one runtime assertion — would have caught this whole class of bug at the test-discipline level); extract the two strict schemas from `bookings.ts` into `shared/schemas/booking.ts` so they're reusable across the walkin + add-room + create + reschedule endpoints (a future refactor; would centralize the contract, but the current file-local approach matches the existing per-handler schema convention).
- ✅ **FOL-01 — Booking drawer payment-verified state survives the `payment-confirmed` → `confirmed` lifecycle (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/fol-payment-verified-after-confirm`, per decision #197; v0.264.7 → v0.264.8). **REAL BUG FIX** (operator-reported 2026-08-06): "I received a new booking and I confirmed the payment and confirmed the booking but when I viewed the bookings page in the booking drawer folio, the payment was not confirmed, can you check why is this? there should be one source of truth right?" The pre-FOL-01 admin booking drawer's Folio + Overview "Pending" / "Verified" payment badges both read `status === "payment-confirmed"` directly — a transient state that doesn't survive the `payment-confirmed` → `confirmed` transition. So a booking the staff had already verified (the common case after a `Verify & Record Payment` followed by `Confirm Booking`) silently fell through to the "Pending" label in the Folio section, and rendered NO badge at all in the Overview section. The data was consistent (the booking's `paymentConfirmedAt` was set, the `onsitePayments[]` ledger had the verified entry, the `reservations/{id}.paymentStatus` mirror was set); only the UI's read was lossy. **Two related UI bugs from the same root cause.** **(1) Folio (line 5195-5208)** — the outer ternary widened the rendered block to include `status === "confirmed"` (so the "View proof" CTA stayed visible), but the inner ternary `status === "payment-confirmed" ? "Verified" : ... : "Pending"` never got the same widening. So a confirmed booking whose payment was verified earlier showed "Pending" on the proof card. **(2) Overview (line 4803-4811)** — the `status === "payment-confirmed"` badge check meant a `confirmed` booking with a stamped `paymentConfirmedAt` rendered NO badge at all. The staff had to click into the Folio section to see the proof status. **Root cause** — `bookings/{id}.paymentConfirmedAt` is the durable signal: stamped by `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` (line 7700) and `handleMarkPaymentConfirmed` (line 7507) and `handleConfirmBookingWithBalance` (line 8130) on the full-payment transition, and NEVER cleared by any other lifecycle handler. `status === "payment-confirmed"` is a transient state — it only says "right now" — and the lifecycle can move past it without erasing the verification history. The pre-FOL-01 UI read the transient state, not the durable signal. The `onsitePayments[]` ledger entries don't carry a "verified" flag — they're just money-movement rows; the `paymentConfirmedAt` timestamp is the only durable signal for the verification event. The shared `Booking` type didn't even expose the field; the admin mapper silently dropped it on every snapshot echo (the same shape of bug as MRB-15-10, where the room-types hydration dropped `maxChildren` / `maxExtraBeds` / `extraBedRate`). **Fix — five-part change.** **(1) New shared helper `isPaymentVerified(booking)` in `shared/utils/paymentVerification.ts`.** Pure function, returns `true` when `status === "payment-confirmed"` (transient) OR `paymentConfirmedAt` is set (durable). Accepts both ISO-string (admin mapper shape) and `Date` (guest lookup shape) for the timestamp. Defensive against null / undefined / empty-string / whitespace / invalid-Date inputs. **(2) New shared `Booking.paymentConfirmedAt` field** (`Date | null`) declared on the shared type. The field is already on the Firestore doc; the type just doesn't expose it. Mirrors the existing `paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy` cluster. **(3) Admin `Booking` extension declares the same field as `string | null` (the admin's Date → ISO-string convention).** The bookings mapper at `AdminContext.tsx:1362` hydrates it via `parseDateTimeString(data.paymentConfirmedAt)`. The pre-FOL-01 mapper silently dropped the field on every snapshot echo. **(4) Wire the helper into both render sites in `BookingsPage.tsx`.** The Folio block's outer ternary widens to `isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking) || selectedBooking.status === "confirmed" || selectedBooking.paymentRejectionReason`; the inner ternary replaces `status === "payment-confirmed"` with `isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking)`. The Overview's "Pending" badge adds a `!isPaymentVerified(selectedBooking)` guard (so the "Pending" + "Verified" badges are mutually exclusive); the "Verified" badge condition switches from the transient status check to the helper. Both render sites are now driven by the same single source of truth. **(5) Export the helper from `shared/index.ts`** so the admin app imports it through `@spark-inn/shared` (matches the existing `getLatestPaymentReference` import pattern). **What the user sees after the change** — a `confirmed` booking whose payment was verified earlier now reads as "Verified" in the Folio (was "Pending") AND shows the green "Verified" badge in the Overview (was no badge at all). A `payment-confirmed` booking still reads as "Verified" (the helper's transient axis is unchanged for this case). A `payment-uploaded` booking still reads as "Pending" (no verification yet). A `cancelled` booking with no verification still reads as nothing. A legacy `confirmed` booking that was confirmed via the legacy `handleConfirmBooking` shortcut (no `paymentConfirmedAt`, status went `payment-uploaded` → `confirmed` directly) still reads as "Pending" (no verification happened) — the helper is strict on this case. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `paymentConfirmedAt` is already on the Firestore doc; the shared type now exposes it; the admin mapper now hydrates it. The server is untouched. **What this changes for the email + notification side** — nothing. The `payment-confirmed` email + the `payment` notification are still fired by `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` and the related handlers; the helper is read-only. **Implementation** — 1 new helper file (`shared/utils/paymentVerification.ts`, ~120 lines) + 1 new unit-test file (`shared/__tests__/paymentVerification.test.ts`, 24 tests) + 1 new admin source-text test (`admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-01-payment-verified-after-confirm.test.ts`, 10 tests) + 4 modified files (`shared/types/index.ts`, `admin-app/src/context/AdminContext.tsx`, `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx`, `shared/index.ts`). ~80 lines changed across 4 modified files + 3 new files. **Tests** — 24 unit tests in `shared/__tests__/paymentVerification.test.ts` (the helper's full truth table — null / undefined / empty / whitespace / invalid-Date / all status × all timestamp combinations) + 10 source-text guards in `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-01-payment-verified-after-confirm.test.ts` (the shared type declares the field, the shared index exports the helper, the admin mapper hydrates the field via `parseDateTimeString`, the admin `Booking` extension declares the field, the `isPaymentVerified` symbol is on the import line, the Folio's outer ternary includes the helper, the Folio's inner ternary reads through the helper, the Overview's "Verified" badge condition reads through the helper, the Overview's "Pending" badge condition gains the `!isPaymentVerified` guard, the pre-FOL-01 inner-ternary pattern is gone from the Folio's render block). All other admin + shared tests pass (10 source-text failures in `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` / `api-base-url.test.ts` / `dashboard-overdue-checkouts.test.ts` / `booking-room-cart.test.ts` are pre-existing and unrelated to FOL-01, also failing on the dev baseline). **MDs** — spec implicit in this entry + `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #197` (the full decision record). **Rejected** — keep the transient `status === "payment-confirmed"` check but also accept `status === "confirmed"` in the inner ternary (special-cases the confirmed case but doesn't survive further lifecycle moves like `checked-in` / `checked-out`; also doesn't generalize to a future "verified post-confirmation" workflow); add a separate `paymentStatus` field on the booking doc that mirrors the current `status` axis (redundant with `status`; the durable signal is the timestamp, not a derived status); surface a "Payment verified at <timestamp>" subline in the Folio (more information than needed; the "Verified" label is the staff's signal that the lifecycle has the right state); add a server-side `paymentVerified: boolean` field that the lifecycle handlers explicitly flip (more server-side coupling; the timestamp is already a durable signal, no need to add a second one); hydrate `paymentConfirmedAt` in the guest-app `Booking` mapper too (the helper accepts both shapes; the guest lookup doesn't render the admin "Verified" badge so the field is not read on the guest side yet — can be added when needed); wire the helper into the Bookings table status pill (line 1978) so a `confirmed` row's status reads "Verified" not "Confirmed" (the table's status pill is the booking lifecycle, not the payment lifecycle — different axes, leave the table alone). **Gates** — PRC-13 (the verify-and-record-payment modal's success-modal `isFullPayment` math is unchanged; the helper is a UI-side read), MRB-12 (the reservation-header `paymentStatus` mirror is unchanged; the helper is per-child, not reservation-aggregate; the existing reservation-row `paymentStatus` reader at line 1978 is the reservation-level equivalent), CWB (the confirm-with-balance path stamps `paymentConfirmedAt` too — the helper's transient axis is `payment-confirmed`, not `confirmed`, so the CWB-confirmed booking reads as "Verified" via the durable-timestamp axis, not the transient; no double-counting), `handleVerifyAndRecordPayment` (the server-side writer is unchanged; the `paymentConfirmedAt` field is the same `Date` it always wrote), the existing `paymentRejectionReason`-based "Rejected" badge (still reads from the same field; the rejection path is a different axis, unaffected by the verified helper). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in FOL-01)** — hydrate `paymentRejectedAt` + `paymentRejectedBy` in the admin mapper (so the rejected-timestamp surfaces in the audit drawer; not blocking the verified fix); wire the helper into the DrawerWorkspaceHeader's "Reference" fallback (the current "Pending verification" italic text is a different concern — about the reference number, not the verification state; can be updated in a follow-up); add a per-payment-ledger "verified at" timestamp on each row in the folio's payment list (the booking-level `paymentConfirmedAt` is the only verified signal today; per-row verified-at is a future audit refinement); surface the verified-state distinction in the Bookings table status pill (different axis, different scope).

- ✅ **Booking-flow audit + 4-patch hotfix (EXB-10 + EXB-12 resync across reschedule + add-room) (off-roadmap bug fix batch)** (shipped 2026-08-06 on `fix/booking-flow-audit-resync`, no new decision; v0.264.8 → v0.264.9). Post-v0.264.5 booking-flow audit closed out the v0.264.7 test-discipline gap with 4 findings the audit surfaced. The audit mapped the full booking surface (`handleCreateBooking` + `handleCreateWalkin` + `handleRescheduleBooking` + `handleAddRoomToReservation` + the rate-breakdown rebuilds + the strict Zod schemas + the client send shapes + the booking-doc shape + the test discipline) and found 3 real bugs that ship + 1 silent-data-loss gap. All 4 are the same class: "feature X wasn't propagated to path Y." The pre-existing 3 `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` failures are unrelated. **(1) Reschedule dropped the extra-bed breakfast charge.** `handleRescheduleBooking` recomputed `breakfastTotal` via `calculateBreakfastAddOn` but did NOT pass `extraBedCount` / `extraBedBreakfast`. A reschedule of a booking with the EXB-12 toggle on silently lost `rate × extraBedCount × nights` from the total. The guest was undercharged. Fix: pass the two new fields. Pin at the call site via a runtime helper test + a source-text regex test (the v0.264.5 class of regression). **(2) Reschedule dropped the "Extra bed add-on" line from the rate breakdown display.** The reschedule's `buildRateBreakdown` call did NOT pass `extraBedTotal` / `extraBedCount` / `extraBedRate`, so the receipt PDF + email + admin drawer showed NO extra-bed line for rescheduled bookings. The math (`extraBedTotal`) was correctly computed by `calculateExtraBedAddOn` two blocks above — only the display wiring was broken. Fix: pass the three new fields. **(3) Add-room was missing the EXB-10 hotel-wide rollaway inventory check.** The `checkExtraBedInventory` call exists in `handleCreateBooking` (line 2107) + `handleCreateWalkin` (line 4128) + `handleRescheduleBooking` (line 9573) — but NOT in `handleAddRoomToReservation`. The desk could add a 3rd extra-bed room to a 14-room property when the hotel only has 2 rollaway beds across the property. Symptom: a same-day check-in finds zero rollaways at the front desk. Fix: copy the inventory block from the reschedule path. **(4) Add-room child doc missing the `breakfastIncludesChildren` + `extraBedBreakfast` snapshots (silent data loss, P1).** The `newBookingDoc` composition in `handleAddRoomToReservation` did NOT write `breakfastIncludesChildren` (the per-child CHD-10 snapshot) or `extraBedBreakfast` (the per-child EXB-12 snapshot). The create + walkin paths write both. The rate-breakdown rebuild is back-compat (undefined → `false` / `true` defaults) so the math is correct today — but any future read site that does `booking.breakfastIncludesChildren === true` or `booking.extraBedBreakfast === true` would see `undefined` for add-room children, not the inherited value. The per-child snapshot pattern (per CHD-10 / EXB-12 specs) is the *whole point* of the data model — the admin default can change tomorrow but the booking's bill shouldn't. Fix: write `breakfastIncludesChildren: Boolean((reservation as any).breakfastIncludesChildren ?? true)` and `extraBedBreakfast: false`. **Tests — new `guest-app/tests/api/exb-10-12-reschedule-addroom-consistency.test.ts` (18 assertions across 4 patches).** Each patch has TWO layers: a runtime helper test (catches the math bug) and a source-text regex test on the handler call site (catches the v0.264.5 class of "field dropped from the call site" regression). Test fix — `exb-09-rate-snapshotting-and-breakfast-coupling.test.ts`: the pre-EXB-12 EXB-09 test asserted "the breakfast call must NOT include `extraBedCount`" (the EXB-04 contract). EXB-12 changed the contract: extra bed enters the breakfast math ONLY when the `extraBedBreakfast` toggle is on. The test's regex was too narrow (800-char window) and its assertion too strict (`not.toMatch(/extraBedCount/)`). The fix widens the window to 5000 chars and inverts the assertion: "every call site that passes `extraBedCount` MUST also pass `extraBedBreakfast`" (so the helper's gate is always wired). **Full guest-app API suite** — 1421 pass + 3 pre-existing `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` failures (called out in the v0.264.5 commit message; unrelated to this fix). typecheck clean, api-bundle clean. **MDs** — this entry (audit findings live here). **Gates** — EXB-12, EXB-10, CHD-10, EXB-04, MRB-11, MRB-14 all preserved. The v0.264.9 retrofit entry below documents the test-discipline follow-up.

- ✅ **Test-discipline retrofit (v0.264.9 → v0.264.10) (off-roadmap refactor)** (shipped 2026-08-06 on `refactor/test-discipline-runtime-assertions`, no new decision; v0.264.9 → v0.264.10). Post-v0.264.9 test-discipline retrofit. The v0.264.5 audit surfaced the systemic class of bug: a future field added to a client body but missing from the server's strict Zod schema (the EXB-12 `extraBedBreakfast` regression). The pre-retrofit test surface was 100% source-text regex in 79 of 119 test files (66%), including every file that tested wire shapes (schemas, multi-room, add-room, breakfast toggle, picker refactor). The regex never exercised the actual schema objects, so "16/16 tests pass" was meaningless — the v0.264.5 fix had to add the v0.264.7 runtime test AFTER the bug shipped. The retrofit closes the loop at the test-discipline level: each of the 5 highest-risk regex-only test files gets ONE runtime assertion (the source-text regex is kept as a fast contract guard; the runtime assertion is the behavioral guard), AND a new meta-test fails the suite if a retrofit file silently loses its runtime assertion. **The 5 retrofit files** — picked for the highest regression risk. **(1) `exb-12-extra-bed-breakfast.test.ts`** — added a runtime `calculateBreakfastAddOn` call that asserts the EXB-12 toggle delta. **(2) `mrb-06-n-room-create.test.ts`** — added a runtime `createBookingSchema.safeParse` call on a representative N=2 body. **(3) `mrb-11-revenue-allocation-snapshot.test.ts`** — added two runtime `assertBookingRevenueAllocationInvariant` calls (valid round-trip + off-by-1 throw). **(4) `exb-10-bed-inventory.test.ts`** — added three runtime `checkExtraBedInventory` calls (boundary case + over-by-1 + 0 = no constraint). **(5) `chd-11-soft-constraint-picker-and-cart-summary.test.ts`** — added a runtime `parseBookingRoomCart` call that pins the EXB-12 `extraBedBreakfast` field from the URL. **The meta-test — `guest-app/tests/api/_test-discipline-meta.test.ts`.** A new file that hard-codes the list of 5 retrofit files + the contract each one pins. For each entry, the meta-test reads the file from disk and asserts it contains at least one runtime primitive (`vi.`, `safeParse(`, `mockFn`, `import(`, `fetch(`). If a future commit silently removes the runtime assertion from a retrofit file, the meta-test fails loud. A 7th test reports the current state of test discipline (X / Y test files have a runtime primitive, Z%) as a `console.log` — informational, not a fail. The pre-retrofit baseline was ~34% (40 of 119); the post-retrofit is ~37.5% (45 of 120). **Full guest-app API suite** — 1436 pass (up from 1421 post-v0.264.9; +15 new test assertions) + 3 pre-existing `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` failures (unrelated). typecheck clean, api-bundle unchanged. **MDs** — this entry (process change, not a feature). **Phase 2 (deferred)** — extend the retrofit to 10 more files (target: ~50% by end of Q1 2027); add runtime `safeParse` regression tests for `RescheduleBookingSchema` + `AddRoomBookingSchema` (the next highest-risk wire shapes); promote the meta-test to also fail the pre-commit hook; document the "regex + runtime" pattern in `CONTRIBUTING.md`.

- ✅ **CHD-11.5 — Explicit Adults + Children picker (replace Guests stepper with Adults stepper) (off-roadmap UX refinement)** (shipped 2026-08-05 on `feature/chd-11-5-explicit-adults-picker`, per decision #196; v0.263.0 → v0.264.0). Operator-reported 2026-08-05 (post-CHD-11.4 review, with screenshot): "I was thinking of instead of asking the number of guests, lets just ask the number of adults and children." The pre-CHD-11.5 surface asks for "Guests" (the total) + "Children" (a sub-count), with `numAdults` derived as `guests - numChildren`. The user thinks in terms of adults and children but has to translate to "guests." The new shape is direct: the picker asks for Adults + Children, and the total is derived. **Why this is the right shape** — (1) more direct (the user picks the domain model directly: "I have 4 adults and 3 kids"), (2) the "at least 1 adult" rule is enforced at the Adults stepper's min (1), not via the submit gate (the submit gate becomes defense in depth), (3) the 0-adults case is naturally impossible (the picker can't produce an invalid state via the UI), (4) matches the homepage widget (CHD-13) which already has separate Adults + Children steppers, (5) CHD-11.1's auto-bump + CHD-11.3's symmetric auto-bump + CHD-11.4's "submit gate catches 0 adults" are all moot — the picker becomes a "naturally" free expression surface. **Fix — multi-part change to `BookingPage.tsx` + `HomePage.tsx`.** **(1) Replace the "Guests" state with "Adults" state.** `const [guests, setGuests] = useState(...)` becomes `const [adults, setAdults] = useState(...)`. The `numAdults` derivation `Math.max(0, guests - numChildren)` is gone — `numAdults = adults` (the state). The `guests` value (for the server + cart summary) is derived as `const guests = adults + numChildren`. **(2) Rename `updateGuests` → `updateAdults` and `setOccupancy(nextGuests, nextChildren)` → `setOccupancy(nextAdults, nextChildren)`.** The `setOccupancy` body clamps `safeAdults = Math.min(Math.max(nextAdults, 1), maxGuestCapacity)` + `safeChildren = Math.max(0, Math.min(nextChildren, safeAdults))`. The URL write changes `next.set("guests", ...)` to `next.set("adults", ...)`. **(3) Update the URL contract: `?guests=N&children=N` → `?adults=N&children=N`.** New canonical shape. Backward compat: the URL reader at line 214 reads `searchParams.get("adults")` first, falls back to `searchParams.get("guests")` (with `adults = max(1, guests - children)`). When the picker writes the URL, it writes the new contract. **(4) Replace the "Guests" stepper with the "Adults" stepper in the picker UI.** The Adults stepper has min 1 (the "at least 1 adult" rule), max `maxGuestCapacity`. The Children stepper stays. The helper text under Children is updated to reference `adults` instead of `guests`. **(5) Update `selectedMaxSelectableChildren` derivation.** The historical auto-bump scenario is gone — `effectiveGuests = adults + children` is just the total. The derivation simplifies: for each candidate child count `N`, compute overflow with `numAdults = adults` (the user's chosen adults) + `N` children. The highest `N` with `overflow <= maxExtraBeds` is the cap. **(6) Update `HomePage` URL writing.** `searchAvailability` writes `?adults=N&children=N` (no `guests` param). **(7) Update the submit gate's "at least 1 adult" check.** The existing `numAdults >= 1` check at line 724 is now redundant (the picker enforces it) but stays as defense in depth. The per-room `cartDistributionComplete` check at line 463 is unchanged. **What the user sees after the change** — picker has two steppers: "Adults" (1-N) and "Children (0-11)" (0-10). The Children stepper's helper text shows "X adults" inline (same as before, just `X` is now the state). The user's existing screenshot case (17 guests, 10 children, 7 adults) maps to Adults=7, Children=10. Deep link to `?guests=5&children=2` derives `adults = 3` (max(1, 5-2)); the picker shows Adults=3, Children=2. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. `numAdults` state (renamed from `guests - numChildren` derivation) is now the state. `numChildren` state stays. The server-side `numAdults + numChildren === guests` consistency check is preserved. The cart serialization is unchanged. **Implementation** — `guest-app/src/pages/BookingPage.tsx` (~30 lines: state rename + `setOccupancy` rename + picker UI + derivation + URL reader/writer) + `guest-app/src/pages/HomePage.tsx` (~3 lines: URL writing). **Tests** — new `guest-app/tests/api/chd-11-5-explicit-adults-picker.test.ts` (~13 source-text guards). Update 5 prior tests: `chd-05-guest-child-cap.test.ts`, `chd-11-1-picker-auto-bump-guests.test.ts`, `chd-11-2-picker-cap-soft-10.test.ts`, `chd-11-3-symmetric-auto-bump.test.ts`, `chd-11-4-picker-free-expression.test.ts` to reflect the new state shape. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-FLOW.md §CHD-11.5 — Explicit Adults + Children Picker`, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #196`. **Rejected** — keep the "Guests + Children" shape but rename "Guests" to "Adults" (semantically wrong; "Guests" means the total, "Adults" means a sub-count); add a separate "Adults" stepper alongside the "Guests" stepper (overengineered; the Adults picker REPLACES the Guests picker); change the URL contract without backward compat (breaks deep links from CHD-13 + SEO + external links); server-side change to drop the `guests` param (no change needed; the server already takes `numAdults + numChildren`). **Gates** — CHD-05 (server contract unchanged; the client's new shape sends the same data, just `numAdults` is now the state), CHD-11 (the picker is now a true exploration surface; the chip + submit gate are the only validation surfaces), CHD-11.1 (auto-bump reversed by CHD-11.4, moot in CHD-11.5), CHD-11.2 (soft cap of 10 stays), CHD-11.3 (auto-bump reversed by CHD-11.4, moot in CHD-11.5; per-type cap removal stays), CHD-11.4 (the "submit gate catches 0 adults" check is now redundant; stays as defense in depth), CHD-12 (cart summary unchanged), CHD-13 (homepage widget state unchanged; URL writing updated to write `?adults=N&children=N`), EXB-11 + EXB-11.1 + EXB-11.2 (extras toggle re-fires when `numAdults` or `numChildren` change), Step 2/3 aside (unchanged), MRB-15-10 + MRB-15-11 (unrelated), EXB-11.3 (independent). **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in CHD-11.5)** — rename the homepage widget's "Guests" trigger button to "People" or "Travelers"; add a "Reset to 1 adult + 0 children" quick action; show the total `guests = adults + children` in the picker UI; server-side allow `numAdults: 0` per-room (relax CHD-05); a "0 adults + N children" preset button (out of scope; the new picker enforces min 1 at the Adults stepper).

- ✅ **FOL-02 — Admin bookings mapper missing-field hydration (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-07 on `fix/fol-02-admin-bookings-mapper-missing-reservation-cluster`, per decision #198; v0.264.10 → v0.264.11). **REAL BUG FIX** (operator-reported 2026-08-07, post-FOL-01). After FOL-01 fixed the Overview + Folio "Verified" badge, the staff saw a new desync in the same drawer: the booking was `confirmed` + `paymentConfirmedAt` was set + the verified payment was in the canonical subcollection, but the Folio's "Payment history" was empty, "PAID ₱0", the "Collect ₱3,900" button was still on screen, and the header's "Reference" line said "Pending verification." The data was correct; the admin's read layer was lossy. **Three related bugs from the same root cause — the admin bookings mapper at `AdminContext.tsx:1335-1448` silently dropped 8 contract fields on every snapshot echo** (the same shape of bug as FOL-01 / MRB-15-10 — the type declares a field, the mapper forgets to read it from the snapshot). **(1) `reservationId` (and the rest of the MRB-01 cluster)** — the booking drawer's payments listener at `BookingsPage.tsx:1164-1224` gates on `selectedBooking.reservationId` to decide whether to subscribe to the post-MRB-01 canonical `reservations/{id}/payments/` path or the legacy `bookings/{id}/payments/` path. With the field always `null` in React state (mapper dropped it), every booking fell through to the legacy path — so a verified payment written to the canonical subcollection was invisible to the Folio's "Payment history", the `paymentsTotal` was `0`, the `balance` was `grandTotal`, and the "Collect <balance>" CTA stayed on screen even after the staff verified the full amount. **(2) `onsitePayments`** — the header's "Reference" line and the Folio's proof card "gcash · <reference>" line read `getLatestPaymentReference(booking)` which returned `null` for new bookings because the denormalized array is empty for new payments (the server's verify / add-payment handlers write to the subcollection, not the array — the array is a pre-MRB-04 relic that was never wired to the new write path). The staff saw "Pending verification" forever. The Bookings table's "PAID" pill at `BookingsPage.tsx:2074` reduced over the empty array and rendered ₱0 paid for every row; the advanced filter's "Reference" search at line 1759 matched against the empty array and returned no results. **(3) `paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy`** — the dashboard's reject-payment card read `paymentRejectionReason` from the unhydrated field and silently lost the rejection reason on every snapshot echo. **Root cause** — same shape of bug as FOL-01 (just shipped) and MRB-15-10 (the room-types hydration dropped `maxChildren` / `maxExtraBeds` / `extraBedRate`). The admin `Booking` extension declared the fields but the mapper never read them from the snapshot. The pre-MRB-01 to pre-MRB-04 / pre-MRB-15-10 era the mapper grew organically and the contract changed under it; FOL-02 is the cleanup that pins the contract at the source level. **Fix — three-part change.** **(1) Hydrate the 5 missing fields in the mapper at `AdminContext.tsx`.** The MRB-01 reservation cluster (`reservationId` / `reservationRef` / `reservationPosition` / `reservationRoomCount`) with the same `String().trim()` / `Number.isFinite()` normalization the writers use. The `onsitePayments` array with per-entry normalization (`id` is `String()`, `amount` is `Number()`, `recordedAt` handles Date + Firestore Timestamp + ISO string). The default for `onsitePayments` is `[]` (never `undefined`) so the read is always safe. **(2) Hydrate the 3 rejection fields** (`paymentRejectionReason` / `paymentRejectedAt` / `paymentRejectedBy`) — same `parseDateTimeString` convention as `paymentConfirmedAt` from FOL-01. Ships as a single bundle so the next mirror-bug (a future dashboard card reading a different field that the mapper also drops) doesn't bite tomorrow. **(3) Wire the live reference lookup.** A new `selectedBookingLatestReference` `useMemo` in `BookingsPage.tsx` computes the reference from the live subcollection listener state (preferred) + the booking's denormalized `onsitePayments[]` array (fallback). Pattern matches the `getBookingFolio` helper's live-vs-persisted disambiguation. The new `latestPaymentReference` prop on `BookingDrawerWorkspaceHeader` consumes the memo; the Folio's proof card consumes the same memo. The pre-FOL-02 inline `getLatestPaymentReference(selectedBooking)` call is gone from the Folio's render block. **What the user sees after the change** — a `confirmed` booking with a verified payment now shows: Folio "Payment history" populated from the subcollection, "PAID ₱3,900" (was "PAID ₱0"), "BALANCE ₱0" (was "₱3,900"), no "Collect" button (was "Collect ₱3,900"), header "Reference: <ref>" (was "Pending verification"), proof card "gcash · <ref>" (was "gcash · No reference"). The Bookings table's "PAID" pill now reads the actual paid amount; the advanced filter's "Reference" search now matches. The dashboard's reject-payment card now shows the rejection reason after a staff reject. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. All 8 fields are already on the Firestore doc; the mapper just wasn't reading them. The server is untouched. **What this changes for the email + notification side** — nothing. The mapper is a read path. **Implementation** — ~80 lines changed across 3 modified files (`AdminContext.tsx` mapper + `BookingDrawerWorkspace.tsx` header + `BookingsPage.tsx` memo + JSX) + 2 new test files (~24 tests total). **Tests** — 14 source-text guards in `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-admin-bookings-mapper-missing-fields.test.ts` (the MRB-01 cluster is hydrated with the correct normalization, the `onsitePayments` array is hydrated with per-entry normalization, the rejection cluster is hydrated with the FOL-01 `parseDateTimeString` convention, the FOL-01 `paymentConfirmedAt` hydration is preserved (no regression on the prior fix), the Bookings table's `PAID` pill still reads from `row.onsitePayments`, the payments listener's path-routing still gates on `selectedBooking.reservationId` and subscribes to `reservations/{id}/payments/` for new bookings) + 10 source-text guards in `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-live-reference-wiring.test.ts` (the `selectedBookingLatestReference` memo prefers the live state + falls back to the persisted array, the memo's dep array is correct, the header's new `latestPaymentReference` prop is on the interface + destructure + JSX, the header uses `latestPaymentReference ?? getLatestPaymentReference(booking)` as the primary read, the parent wires the memo into the header, the Folio's proof card uses the memo, the pre-FOL-02 inline call is gone from the Folio's render block). All other admin + shared tests pass (10 pre-existing failures in `bookings-staff-email-dedup.test.ts` / `booking-room-cart.test.ts` / `api-base-url.test.ts` / `dashboard-overdue-checkouts.test.ts` are unrelated to FOL-02, also failing on the dev baseline). **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md §FOL-02` (added at the bottom of the spec), decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #198`, pattern documented in `plan/docs/GOTCHAS.md` as an extension of the FOL-01 "transient vs durable signal" gotcha (the "field-drop on read" pattern is the same shape as MRB-15-10). **Rejected** — hydrate only `reservationId` and ship the rest as a follow-up (half-fix; the user would discover the same bug pattern tomorrow on `onsitePayments` + the next day on the rejection cluster; the single-bundle pattern matches MRB-15-10's "pin every contract field" fix); denormalize the payments ledger into the booking's `onsitePayments[]` array server-side as well (broader scope than needed; the read layer can prefer the live state + fall back to the array; the server's `bookings/{id}/payments` vs `reservations/{id}/payments` split is the canonical design per MRB-04); modify the shared `getLatestPaymentReference` helper to accept a second `livePayments` parameter (cleaner shared API but couples the helper to the live-vs-persisted disambiguation, which is an admin concern; the local memo + prop pattern keeps the helper pure); add a server-side sync from the canonical subcollection to the booking's `onsitePayments[]` array on every verify / add-payment (more server-side coupling for a problem the read layer can solve); hydrate every other missing field on the admin `Booking` extension (`rateBreakdown` / `memberDiscountPct` / `pointsAwarded` / `confirmedWithBalance` / `unpaidCheckoutReason` / `rescheduleHistory` / `isTestData` / `earlyCheckIn` / etc.) — out of scope for the visible bug; can ship as FOL-03 in a future pass if any of these bite. **Gates** — MRB-01 (the contract is preserved end-to-end; the new bookings + the admin surface + the deep-link resolution all read the same four fields from the snapshot; legacy null-`reservationId` bookings keep today's self-contained behavior), MRB-04 (the canonical vs legacy subcollection split is preserved; the mapper hydration makes the listener correctly subscribe to the canonical path for new bookings; the legacy path stays correct for legacy bookings), MRB-12 (the reservation-header `paymentStatus` mirror is unchanged; the helper is per-child, not reservation-aggregate; the existing reservation-row `paymentStatus` reader at line 1978 is the reservation-level equivalent), PRC-13 (the verify-and-record-payment modal's success-modal `isFullPayment` math is unchanged), the live reference lookup is consistent with the existing `getBookingFolio` helper's live-vs-persisted disambiguation. **Phase 2 (deferred, NOT in FOL-02)** — hydrate the rest of the missing fields on the admin `Booking` extension (`rateBreakdown` / `memberDiscountPct` / `pointsAwarded` / `pendingLoyaltyPoints` / `loyaltyAwardStatus` / `pointsAwardedAt` / `checkedOutWithBalance` / `checkedOutFolioTotal` / `checkedOutCollectedTotal` / `earlyCheckoutOriginalCheckOut` / `confirmedWithBalance` / `confirmedWithBalanceReason` / `confirmedWithBalanceAt` / `confirmedWithBalanceBy` / `unpaidCheckoutReason` / `unpaidCheckoutApprovedBy` / `rescheduleHistory` / `isTestData` / `testRunId` / `earlyCheckIn`) — out of scope for the visible bug; can ship as FOL-03 in a future pass if any of these bite; denormalize the payments ledger into the booking's `onsitePayments[]` array server-side on every verify / add-payment (broader scope; would need a Firestore trigger or a write-path migration; the read layer fix is enough for the visible bug).
- ✅ **Check-in readiness card hides after check-in (off-roadmap UI fix)** (shipped 2026-08-07 on `fix/checkin-readiness-card-stale-after-checkin`, no new decision; v0.264.12 → v0.264.13). Operator-reported 2026-08-07 (post-FOL-03): after tapping "Verify guest ID & check in" the admin drawer still showed the "Check-in readiness" amber card with "1 missing: Booking status must be confirmed or payment-confirmed" — confusing because the guest was clearly already checked in. **Root cause** — the `<BookingCheckInReadiness>` card at `BookingsPage.tsx:4884` was rendered unconditionally whenever the drawer was open. The `getCheckInReadiness` helper correctly reports `"checked-in"` as not in `CHECK_IN_ELIGIBLE_STATUSES`, so once the staff flips the status the helper flips the card into the same "you're missing something" amber state it was warning about pre-check-in. The gate is only meaningful BEFORE the transition; the same booking-status check the card is showing already passed in the post-check-in state. The "Verify guest ID & check in" button itself was already correctly gated to `confirmed` / `payment-confirmed` (only shown for those two statuses) — the card just didn't share that lifecycle. **Fix — one-gate change to `BookingsPage.tsx:4884`.** Import `CHECK_IN_ELIGIBLE_STATUSES` from `@spark-inn/shared` (already exported from `shared/utils/checkin.ts`) and wrap the `<BookingCheckInReadiness>` render in `CHECK_IN_ELIGIBLE_STATUSES.includes(selectedBooking.status as ...)`. The card now only shows when the gate is actually relevant; after check-in / checkout / cancel the card disappears. The registration form, guest ID photo, lifecycle indicator, and the rest of the check-in section still work as before. The shared `getCheckInReadiness` helper is untouched (still the authoritative gate the server-side check-in handler + the check-in button read) — this is purely a display-side lifecycle alignment. **What the user sees after the change** — opens a confirmed booking → "Check-in readiness" card shows the missing items OR a green "Ready" pill (unchanged). Taps "Verify guest ID & check in" → booking flips to `checked-in`, card disappears, lifecycle indicator in the header reflects the new status, registration form + ID photo remain visible for the post-check-in audit. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. The writes are the same; only the card's render condition changed. **Implementation** — `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx` (1 import addition, 1 render guard, 1 explanatory comment block; ~15 lines changed total) + `admin-app/src/__tests__/checkin-gate.test.ts` (1 new test, 11 lines). **Tests** — the existing `checkin-gate.test.ts` source-text guards continue to pass; new test pins the gate-to-pre-check-in behavior so a future regression (showing the card for `checked-in`) fails loud. All other admin + shared tests pass. `tsc --noEmit` clean for `admin-app`. **MDs** — this entry. **Rejected** — change `getCheckInReadiness` to return `ready: true` for `checked-in` (wrong layer — would silently say "ready" for `cancelled` bookings too; the helper is the server-side gate and is correct as-is); move the check-in button's enabled-state to the same `missingItems` list (the button already has a tighter display-side check tied to `confirmed` / `payment-confirmed`; aligning the card to the same axis is the right move); hide the entire check-in section post-check-in (the registration form + ID photo are useful post-check-in audit surfaces — the card is the only piece that lies). **Gates** — the check-in button's `disabled={!selectedBookingCheckInReadiness?.ready}` (still correct; the card's `ready` is now the same gate the button reads, just hidden), the server-side `handleCheckinBooking` (unchanged; the helper is still the source of truth, the display is just lifecycle-aligned), the Phase 12 booking-drawer UX test surface (unchanged; the new render condition still satisfies the existing `disabled={!selectedBookingCheckInReadiness?.ready}` regex guard), the existing `getCheckInReadiness` source-text contract (unchanged; the helper is the contract, this is the display).

- ✅ **FOL-03 — Check-in / check-out `runTransaction` reads-before-writes ordering (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-07 on `fix/fol-03-checkin-checkout-transaction-read-order`, per decision #199; v0.264.11 → v0.264.12). **REAL BUG FIX** (operator-reported 2026-08-07, post-FOL-02). The staff saw a 500 on `POST /api/bookings/checkin` for a confirmed + payment-confirmed booking: `Error: Firestore transactions require all reads to be executed before all writes.` The check-in flow died in `handleCheckinBooking` before the booking's status flipped to `checked-in` and the room's status flipped to `occupied`; the same shape of bug lived in `handleCheckoutBooking` and would have fired the moment a guest tried to check out. **The bug shape — Firestore's `runTransaction` requires every `await transaction.get(...)` to complete BEFORE any `transaction.update(...)` / `transaction.set(...)` call, and a transaction that mixes them throws with the canonical error.** The pre-FOL-03 `handleCheckinBooking` and `handleCheckoutBooking` both read the booking doc, the room doc, and the "other check-ins in this reservation" doc first, then started a write (`transaction.update(bookingRef, ...)`), then read the "all children of this reservation" doc for the `checkedInRoomCount` + `paymentStatus` mirror — exactly the reads-after-writes shape the SDK forbids. The read needed to compute the post-update mirror was placed AFTER the writes that mutate the child statuses (the very thing the mirror needs to count), which is structurally impossible to satisfy with a single transaction. **Fix — three-part change to `bookings.ts`.** **(1) Pre-read the children-for-count snapshot BEFORE any write** — the read is hoisted out of the post-write block into the pre-write block in both `handleCheckinBooking` (around line 8256) and `handleCheckoutBooking` (around line 8433). The read's result is stored in a new `childrenForCount` local. **(2) Compute the post-update child statuses in a separate `postUpdateChildStatuses` array** — built from the pre-read's docs with a per-doc ternary that replaces the target booking's status with its post-update value (`"checked-in"` for checkin, `"checked-out"` for checkout) while leaving the other children's pre-update statuses untouched. The mirror computation now reads from this array (filter `s === "checked-in"` for `checkedInRoomCount`, filter `s === "checked-out"` for `newCheckedOutCount` in checkout, then derive `paymentStatus` via `computeReservationAggregatePaymentStatus(postUpdateChildStatuses)`). **(3) Apply all writes in a single contiguous write block AFTER the reads** — the booking + room (and, for checkout, the intercom thread + the reservation header) updates are placed after the pre-reads; the reservation header's `checkedInRoomCount` / `paymentStatus` mirror write uses the pre-computed `postUpdateChildStatuses` so the transaction remains valid. **Why this is the right shape** — (1) atomicity is preserved (the whole point of `runTransaction` per MRB-15-03 — the booking + room + reservation-header mirror still flip together or not at all), (2) the SDK's "all reads first" requirement is now satisfied structurally (no human reviewer can accidentally re-introduce a read-after-write), (3) the post-update state is computed deterministically from the pre-read + the per-doc ternary (no double-read, no stale cache), (4) the variable name `postUpdateChildStatuses` makes the "pre-read + post-update replacement" semantic explicit (the SDK doesn't let us observe the post-update state via a real read, so we have to construct it). **What the user sees after the change** — check-in on a confirmed + payment-confirmed booking returns 200, the booking flips to `checked-in`, the room flips to `occupied`, the reservation header's `checkedInRoomCount` increments correctly, and `paymentStatus` is recomputed. Check-out is the same shape with `"checked-out"`. The error toast that fired on every pre-FOL-03 check-in / check-out click is gone. **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. The writes are the same; only the order changed. The reads are the same; only their position relative to the writes changed. The mirror math is the same; only its source changed (from a now-impossible read to a pre-computed array). **What this changes for the test surface** — the FOL-03 source-text test pins the new ordering; the 2 affected MRB test files (`mrb-15-03-transactional-counters.test.ts` + `mrb-05-aggregate-and-handler-mirror.test.ts`) are updated to reference `postUpdateChildStatuses` instead of the old `childStatuses` name in 7 places total. **Implementation** — ~30 lines changed in `bookings.ts` (the pre-read hoist + the per-doc ternary + the new var name in the mirror write) + 1 new test file (9 source-text guards) + 2 modified test files (7 var-name references). **Tests** — 9 source-text guards in `guest-app/tests/api/fol-03-checkin-checkout-transaction-read-order.test.ts` (the new pre-read appears before the first write in `handleCheckinBooking`; the new pre-read appears before the first write in `handleCheckoutBooking`; `postUpdateChildStatuses` is constructed via the per-doc ternary that replaces the target booking's status; the mirror write in `handleCheckinBooking` reads from `postUpdateChildStatuses`; the mirror write in `handleCheckoutBooking` reads from `postUpdateChildStatuses` for BOTH `newCheckedInCount` AND `newCheckedOutCount`; the pre-FOL-03 read-after-write pattern is gone from both handlers; the test slice boundaries correctly bound the `handleCheckinBooking` body to the `handleCheckoutBooking` declaration and the `handleCheckoutBooking` body to the `handleLookupBooking` declaration so the test reads only the relevant code; the 2 MRB test files reference the new var name). The check-in / check-out end-to-end behaviour tests (`bookings-checkin.test.ts` + `bookings-checkout.test.ts` + `bookings-checkout-archive.test.ts`) already cover the round-trip; the FOL-03 source-text pin is the cheap, deterministic guard against future reordering. No new runtime tests added. All other guest + admin + shared tests pass (the 3 pre-existing BF-04 failures in the test discipline retrofit are unrelated to FOL-03, also failing on the dev baseline). **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md §FOL-03`, this decision entry, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry, ✅ shipped 2026-08-07), pattern documented in `plan/docs/GOTCHAS.md` as an extension of the FOL-01 / FOL-02 entries (the "Firestore `runTransaction` requires all reads before all writes" gotcha). **Rejected** — split the read-then-mirror-write into a second transaction after the booking + room writes commit (loses atomicity — the whole point of MRB-15-03; a crash between the two transactions leaves the booking `checked-in` with a stale `checkedInRoomCount`); use a Firestore `writeBatch` for the writes and a separate query for the mirror read (batches don't support transactional reads; the mirror needs to be atomic with the booking + room writes); reorder the writes to do the reservation header first, then the booking + room (wrong — the count derives from the booking status flip, not the other way around; the order is booking → room → header mirror); add a "post-write re-read" workaround that suppresses the SDK error (the SDK requirement is structural, not a warning; a workaround would be a future regression time-bomb); pre-compute the mirror on the client and pass it in the request body (couples the server to the client's read of the children collection; defeats the server's role as the source of truth; the pre-read-in-transaction is the canonical Firestore pattern). **Gates** — MRB-15-03 (the transactional counters are still atomic — the booking + room + reservation header still flip together; only the read order changed), MRB-01 (the reservation header is still the aggregation target; the `checkedInRoomCount` / `paymentStatus` mirror is still written in the same transaction), PRC-13 (unrelated; verify-and-record-payment doesn't touch the checkin/checkout handlers), the FOL-01 / FOL-02 read-side fixes are unaffected (the mapper hydration is independent of the server's transaction shape).

- ✅ **FOL-04 — My-booking payment-method label is dynamic (off-roadmap bug fix)** (shipped 2026-08-07 on `fix/my-booking-payment-method-dynamic-label`, per decision #200). **REAL BUG FIX** (visual review 2026-08-07, post-FOL-03). The payment-method label on the public `/my-booking` single-booking card + the reservation-scope card was hardcoded in each page's own `paymentLabels` / `legacy` object literal — the same drift the booking confirmation page had until its dynamic fetch landed. When the admin renamed a method's `label` in Settings → Payment Methods (e.g. `gcash` → "GCash Only"), the booking page picked up the new label immediately (it reads `pm.label` from the array dynamically) but the my-booking page kept the legacy "Digital Wallet (GCash/Maya)" — guests saw two different labels for the same booking across the two surfaces. When the admin added a custom method (`pesonet` / `credit-card` / `crypto`), the my-booking + reservation cards fell through to the raw method key because the hardcoded map had no entry, so the guest saw "pesonet" / "credit-card" as the label. **The reservation-scope card was even worse** — it just dumped the raw `paymentMethod` key directly, so every guest with an N-room reservation saw "gcash" / "pesonet" / etc. as the label regardless of how the admin had named it. **Root cause** — each guest-facing page owned its own label map. The booking page (`BookingPage.tsx`) read `pm.label` from the admin config dynamically; the booking confirmation page (`BookingConfirmPage.tsx`) + the my-booking page (`BookingLookupPage.tsx`) carried independent hardcoded maps. The three surfaces could (and did) drift. **Fix — three-part change.** **(1) New shared helper `resolvePaymentMethodLabel(methodKey, paymentMethods)` in `shared/utils/paymentMethodLabel.ts`** — single source of truth for the guest-facing label. Resolution order: (a) match by `method` in the admin-supplied `paymentMethods[]` array → return that entry's `label`; (b) fall through to the `LEGACY_PAYMENT_METHOD_LABELS` map (`gcash` / `bank` / `pay-at-hotel`) — the three keys the booking + corporate + store flows used before the dynamic array existed; (c) fall through to the raw `methodKey` as a last resort. The helper is defensive about input shape (nullish / non-string / non-array / missing `label` field) so a malformed admin doc never throws. **(2) `BookingLookupPage.tsx` now fetches `settings/hotelConfig` and resolves the label dynamically** — a new `useEffect` fires the `getDoc` on `doc(db, "settings", "hotelConfig")` when a result card becomes visible (single OR reservation-scope), is a no-op when the search form is alone, and stores the filtered `paymentMethods[]` in local state. The single-booking card + the reservation-scope card both render through the new helper, so the two cards can never drift apart. **(3) `BookingConfirmPage.tsx` refactored to use the same helper** — the previous in-page `legacy` map and the `dynamicPaymentMethodLabel` / `fallbackPaymentMethodLabel` ternaries are replaced by a single `dynamicPaymentMethods` state that feeds the same helper. The two pages now read from one shared legacy map; renaming `gcash` to "GCash Only" on the admin side flips the label on the confirmation page + the my-booking page + the my-booking reservation card in one shot. **What the user sees after the change** — for the current admin config the cards still show "Digital Wallet (GCash/Maya)" (the legacy map carries the copy). After the admin renames `gcash` → "GCash Only" in Settings, the my-booking card + the reservation card + the booking confirmation all show "GCash Only" on the next page load. After the admin adds a custom `pesonet` method, the cards show "PESONET Bank Transfer" (or whatever the admin typed) instead of the raw "pesonet". **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. The booking doc's `paymentMethod` field is unchanged; the admin's `paymentMethods[]` array is unchanged. Only the render path changed. **What this changes for the server** — nothing. The fix is fully client-side. The shared helper lives in `shared/utils/` so future server-side label rendering (e.g. an admin-side "guest-facing label preview" in Settings) can reuse it. **Implementation** — `shared/utils/paymentMethodLabel.ts` (new, ~80 lines; the helper + the `LEGACY_PAYMENT_METHOD_LABELS` map); `shared/index.ts` (1 line — re-export the helper); `shared/__tests__/paymentMethodLabel.test.ts` (new, 9 behavioural unit tests covering the admin-wins / legacy-fallback / raw-key / empty-label / nullish-input / non-array / non-string-method cases); `guest-app/src/pages/BookingLookupPage.tsx` (replaced the hardcoded `paymentLabels` map with a `resolveLabel` closure; added a `useEffect` that fetches `hotelConfig.paymentMethods[]` when a result card becomes visible; the single-booking card + the reservation-scope card both render through `resolveLabel`); `guest-app/src/pages/BookingConfirmPage.tsx` (replaced the in-page `legacy` map + the ternaries with a single `dynamicPaymentMethods` state that feeds the same shared helper). ~50 lines changed across 2 page files + 1 new shared util + 1 new test file. **Tests** — 9 behavioural unit tests in `shared/__tests__/paymentMethodLabel.test.ts` (admin label wins over the legacy map for a shared key; admin-configured label is returned for a matching method; falls through to the legacy map when the method is not in `paymentMethods[]`; falls through to the raw key when no admin + no legacy match; ignores admin entries with a missing/empty `label` field so a malformed doc never poisons the fallback; returns empty string for empty / non-string / nullish `methodKey`; does not throw on non-array / malformed `paymentMethods` input; ignores admin entries whose `method` is not a string). All other shared + guest + admin tests pass (`tsc -b` clean for both apps). The existing API lookup tests (`bookings-lookup.test.ts` + `bookings-lookup-list.test.ts` + `mrb-10-reservation-lookup.test.ts`) continue to pass — the API contract is unchanged; the label resolution is purely a render-side concern. **MDs** — spec in `plan/features/BOOKING-LOOKUP.md §FOL-04` (new section at the bottom of the spec) + the `paymentMethod` row in the data/logic checklist, decision in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #200`, this entry, pattern documented in `plan/docs/GOTCHAS.md` as an extension of the existing "hardcoded UI label vs admin-editable config" gotcha (decision #108 / SETTINGS.md §Payment Methods already established `paymentMethods[]` as the canonical source; FOL-04 extends the contract enforcement to the public lookup surface). **Rejected** — fetch the label server-side and include it in the `POST /api/bookings/lookup` response (the lookup endpoint is privacy-sensitive — adding a settings read into it would couple a public-facing credentialed endpoint to the admin's settings path, broadening the read blast-radius for no privacy gain;
