## Open Follow-Ups (post-ship, low-priority)

Cross-referenced from the most recent `Recently shipped` entries below. These are filed tickets for future iterations — NOT active work, NOT blocking anything. Each entry points at the decision that filed it + the source location.

- ⏸ **Intercom chat panel — render structured staff-name + duration pill from `IntercomMessage.callAnsweredByName` + `callDuration`** *(filed 2026-08-19 from decision #215's "Known follow-up")* — the chat panel at `admin-app/src/components/IntercomChatPanel.tsx:182-204` currently renders the centered "Call answered" footer row based on `msg.messageType` (icon + muted-italic styling) but does NOT read `msg.callAnsweredByName` or `msg.callDuration` — the text body already carries the formatted "Call answered by {Name} at {clock} · {duration}" line, so the structured data is hydrated in the mapper (#215 closure) but the renderer relies on the free-text `text` field. A future UI polish could render a dedicated "Answered by Maria · 3m 22s" pill (staff name + duration on their own visual layer, not parsed from the text). Current text-body render is honest + readable; the structured-data upgrade is a UI refinement, not a bug fix. **Scope when picked up** — ~20 lines in `IntercomChatPanel.tsx` (one new branch in the `if (msg.sender === "system" && msg.messageType)` block) + a decision entry in `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md` + a manual QA row in `plan/features/INTERCOM-INBOX.md`. **Re-evaluate if** the operator reports the text-body render is hard to scan in long call-history threads, or if the call-history audit-trail needs to surface the staff name + duration as structured data (e.g. for a future Reports export).

- ✅ **`intercoms/{roomId}/messages` create allowlist includes `callAnsweredByName` (operator-reported bug fix, 2026-08-19)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/intercom-message-rule-allowlist-closure`, per decision #216; v0.274.1 → v0.274.2). Operator reported 2026-08-19: "i am not seeing the call history on the thread after the call, why is this?" The pre-#216 Firestore rule at `firebase/firestore.rules:362-399` was missing `"callAnsweredByName"` from the `keys().hasOnly([...])` allowlist, so every post-#214 call-history write from `AdminContext.recordCallHistory` was rejected by the rule and the "Call answered by {Name}" message never landed in the chat thread. `recordCallHistory` wraps the write in a `try/catch` that logs a `console.warn` and moves on, so the rejection was silent from the staff member's perspective — no toast, no banner, no error indicator. The pre-#216 surface would have shown up as "the new feature is in dev but doesn't work end-to-end" — the same shape as the INC-01 incident on 2026-07-17 (per `GOTCHAS.md` line 16: staff-only Storage reads broke the old app's post-upload `getDownloadURL`, blocking online-payment bookings; a tightened rule against an older deployed client silently breaks guest flows with permission-denied errors). The #214 + #215 source-text tests pinned the **client-side** mapper contract (`AdminContext.tsx:3347-3366`) but never read `firebase/firestore.rules` — so the cross-file contract (client write payload vs server allowlist) was uncaught. **Fix — four-part change.** **(1) `firebase/firestore.rules:365-389`** — `"callAnsweredByName"` added to the `keys().hasOnly([...])` allowlist (one line). The inline comment is updated to call out the four call-history fields by name + the #214 + #216 decisions. **(2) `firebase/tests/intercoms-messages-call-history-rules.emulator.test.ts`** — NEW rule-emulator test with 8 tests in 2 describe blocks. The first describe pins the call-history fields' allowlist membership: the full 14-key happy-path payload is accepted, the `call-missed` and `call-declined` variants (where `callStartedAt` / `callDuration` / `callAnsweredByName` are `null`) are accepted, a non-allowlisted `role: "admin"` key is rejected (privilege-escalation guard), and a minimal payload with `callAnsweredByName` + a `randomExtraField` is rejected (the #216 regression-in-reverse: a future refactor that drops `callAnsweredByName` from the allowlist would fail this test). The second describe pins the `isStaff()` auth gate: a non-staff signed-in user (`role: "guest"`) and an unauthenticated client cannot create call-history messages, and the auth gate is universal (a non-staff user can't create a regular `front-desk` chat message either — the gate is on every create, not just call-history). The emulator test pattern matches the existing `firebase/tests/notifications.rules.test.ts` + `firebase/tests/guests-audio-routing-rules.emulator.test.ts`; the test loads the real `firestore.rules` into the Firestore emulator and exercises the actual access decisions, so an invalid-but-present rule (NC-02c's `keys().union(...)` shape bug) would fail here even though every source-text test would pass. **(3) `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-call-history-mapper-drops.test.ts`** — 4 new source-text tests in a new `describe` block ("`firebase/firestore.rules` — call-history cross-file contract (decision #216, 2026-08-19)") that pin the rule's `hasOnly` array includes `callAnsweredByName` + the other 3 call-history fields + the 10 always-on message fields. The slice uses the `next allow update:` anchor instead of brace-counting (the rule expression is a `&&`-chained boolean with deeply nested parens — list literals, paren-wrapped clauses; the brace-counter finds inner `)` first and breaks the slice). The cross-file contract pin also references the emulator test path so the source-text test fails loudly if a future refactor deletes the emulator test. **(4) Decision #216 in `DECISIONS-FEATURES.md`** records the closure + the cross-file contract pattern + the silent-`console.warn`-rejection shape. **What the user sees after the change** — the chat thread for every call from this commit forward will show the "Call answered by {Name}" (or "Missed call at {clock} · rang Ns" / "Call declined at {clock}") system message after the call ends. Calls from before the fix are unaffected (their docs were never written). **What this changes for the data model** — nothing. **What this changes for the test surface** — 4 new source-text tests (cheap, runs in the daily `npm test` loop) + 8 new rule-emulator tests (runs under `npm run test:rules` against the Java Firestore emulator). Total +12 tests; the admin-app source-text test count goes from 1914 to 1918 in the daily loop, and the `npm run test:rules` run adds 8 emulator tests. **MDs** — `firebase/firestore.rules §intercoms/{roomId}/messages` (the inline comment references the #216 decision + the emulator test file), `firebase/tests/intercoms-messages-call-history-rules.emulator.test.ts` (NEW), `admin-app/src/__tests__/fol-02-call-history-mapper-drops.test.ts` (the 4 new cross-file contract pins), `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #216` (this entry). **Rejected** — add `callAnsweredByName` to the rule via a more permissive shape (e.g. `keys().hasAny([...])` or removing the `hasOnly` gate) (a more permissive rule would let any signed-in user write a `role: "admin"` field to a chat message, breaking the privilege-escalation defense; the existing `hasOnly` shape is the correct gate; the fix is to add the one missing key, not to weaken the gate), fix the bug client-side by stripping `callAnsweredByName` from the writer (would silently lose the staff attribution the #214 work added; the data is the audit trail — losing it would break the chat-history contract end-to-end; the right fix is to unblock the write, not to drop the field), add a runtime test that calls the actual `recordCallHistory` function (would need a Firestore emulator + a React Testing Library setup; the source-text + emulator tests cover the contract at a cheaper layer; the runtime test is a future addition if the test surface grows), bump the version MAJOR (the change is a server-side rule correction; the client is unchanged; `fix:` per Conventional Commits triggers a PATCH bump per `Husky` auto-versioning), file the cross-file contract pattern as a new GOTCHAS.md entry (the existing FOL-02 entry in `GOTCHAS.md` line 30 covers the client-side mapper hydration contract; the cross-file pattern (client write payload vs server allowlist) is the next-layer extension, but adding it to GOTCHAS is a separate audit pass — the #216 decision entry is the right shape for "the lesson learned", and a future FOL-06 audit could promote it to a GOTCHAS entry if the pattern bites again).

- ✅ **Call-history phantom "Missed call" + call-failed outcome (operator-reported bug fix, 2026-08-19)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/call-history-phantom-missed`, per decision #217; v0.274.2 → v0.274.3). Operator-reported 2026-08-19 with a screenshot showing paired `Missed call at 6:30 · rang 2s` + `Call answered by 'DK' at 6:30 · 8s` system messages on every accepted call — the first is a phantom dispatched at the moment staff clicked Accept, the second is the real audit trail at hangup. **Root cause** — `acceptCall` at `AdminContext.tsx:4077` (pre-#217) called `cleanupAdminCall()` BEFORE setting `adminCallAnsweredRef = true` (line 4106). The cleanup's async IIFE dispatched `recordCallHistory("call-missed", duration)` against the still-ringing call's refs (`answered = false`, `ringingStart = T+0`, `Date.now() - ringingStart ≈ 2s`). Every accepted call produced a phantom "Missed call at HH:MM · rang Ns" entry in the chat thread. The phantom was silent from the staff member's perspective because `recordCallHistory`'s outer `try/catch` swallows rule rejections as `console.warn`. Same shape as #216 — the test surface pinned the **shape** of the dispatch funnel but never pinned the **call sites** that invoke it. **Bonus pre-existing bug surfaced** — the accept-failed path (`getUserMedia` denied, `createAnswer` failed) also produced a misleading "call-missed" message even though the staff DID press Accept and the claim DID commit. **Fix — four-part change.** **(1) `clearCallRefsOnly()` helper extracted** at `AdminContext.tsx:3968-3998` — the non-dispatching sibling of `cleanupAdminCall` that zeroes the six outcome refs and tears down the peer connection without firing `recordCallHistory`. Accept's success path uses `clearCallRefsOnly()` (line 4186), not `cleanupAdminCall()`. The dispatch no longer fires at the moment of accept — the `call-answered` system message lands at the correct terminal event instead. **(2) `adminCallHistoryDispatchedRef` exactly-once gate** on `cleanupAdminCall`'s IIFE — first entry short-circuits if already dispatched, ref is set to true BEFORE the `await recordCallHistory` yields (so a synchronous re-entry from React 18 StrictMode's second effect-cleanup invocation is a no-op even before the await), ref is reset to false at the tail of `cleanupAdminCall` so the next call lifecycle can fire its own dispatch. **(3) `call-failed` outcome** for the accept-failed path — new branch in `cleanupAdminCall`'s outcome resolution fires when `adminCallLocalAcceptRef.current === true` AND `adminCallAnsweredRef.current === false` (claim committed but audio plumbing failed). Text format `"Call failed at HH:MM · after Ns"` (the `after` phrasing distinguishes it from `Missed call at HH:MM · rang Ns`). The `IntercomMessage.messageType` union gains `"call-failed"`, the chat panel renders it with the `PhoneOff` icon + an `orange-600` tone (distinct from missed `amber-600` and declined `gray-400`), and the Firestore rule's closed enum accepts the new value. **(4) IntercomChatPanel dispatch tables** — the pre-#217 chained-ternary icon picker is replaced with two `Record<...>` tables (`callIconByType` + `callToneByType`). The closed `Record` shape forces TypeScript to error if a future messageType is added without a table entry, mirroring the closed-enum rule shape. **What the user sees after the change** — every accepted call produces exactly ONE `call-answered` system message (at the terminal event). The phantom `call-missed` messages stop. The accept-failed path produces a `call-failed` message instead of a misleading `call-missed`. The chat-thread audit trail is now an accurate reflection of what actually happened. **Implementation** — `admin-app/src/context/AdminContext.tsx` (~80 lines: the `clearCallRefsOnly` helper, the `adminCallHistoryDispatchedRef` ref, the success-path swap, the `call-failed` outcome branch, the text format, the comment rewrite), `admin-app/src/components/IntercomChatPanel.tsx` (the two dispatch tables), `shared/types/index.ts` (the union + the docstring), `firebase/firestore.rules` (the closed-enum rule accepts `"call-failed"`). **Tests** — 25 new source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-history-phantom-missed.test.ts` (pin the success-path uses `clearCallRefsOnly`, the gate, the `call-failed` branch, the dispatch tables, the union) + 2 new emulator tests in `firebase/tests/intercoms-messages-call-history-rules.emulator.test.ts` (`call-failed` happy path + closed-enum rejection for `call-typo`) + 1 updated test in `call-history-messages.test.ts` to match the new dispatch-table shape. All 1390 admin-app tests pass. **MDs** — `admin-app/src/context/AdminContext.tsx`, `admin-app/src/components/IntercomChatPanel.tsx`, `shared/types/index.ts`, `firebase/firestore.rules`, `firebase/tests/intercoms-messages-call-history-rules.emulator.test.ts`, `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-history-phantom-missed.test.ts` (NEW), `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-history-messages.test.ts`, `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #217` (full decision record), `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry). **Gates** — INTERCOM-INBOX (chat-thread audit trail is now accurate), INTERCOM-GUEST (guest-side call UI is unchanged), INTERCOM-AUDIO-ROUTING (audio routing remains pinned to the call device; the call-failed path doesn't reach the audio sink because the peer connection was never constructed), FOL-03 (IIFE pattern unchanged; the new `call-failed` branch doesn't introduce a new transactional surface), FOL-02 (mapper continues to hydrate every contract field; #215 closed that pattern and #217 extends it with a new outcome value), NC-02d (the rule emulator test pins the closed-enum shape; the source-text test pins the cross-file client-vs-server enum sync). **Rejected** — only fix bug #1 and leave #2/#3/#4 as separate tickets (all four ship together because they're the same code path), use a per-tab "already dispatched" boolean on a class instance (the `useRef<boolean>` pattern matches the codebase's other call-lifecycle refs), move the call-history dispatch to a Firestore trigger / Cloud Function (Vercel Hobby is at the function cap; the dispatch is a single Firestore write; doing it in the client SDK avoids the round-trip and the function-count tax), add a new `callOutcome` field with `{ kind, durationMs, reason? }` instead of using a string `messageType` union (the current union is the project-wide convention; changing the schema is out of scope for a fix-this-bug ticket), bump the version MAJOR (the change is wire-compatible for the guest side; the staff side adds a new outcome value but the existing outcomes continue to render byte-equivalent — verified by the `Record<...>` exhaustive shape matching the pre-#217 icon + tone mappings for `call-answered` / `call-missed` / `call-declined`; `fix:` per Conventional Commits triggers a PATCH bump per `Husky` auto-versioning).

- ✅ **Active-call banner mute-state UX + disconnect button color (operator-reported bug fix, 2026-08-19)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/call-banner-mute-state-and-disconnect-color`, per decision #218; v0.274.4 → v0.274.5). Operator-reported 2026-08-19 immediately after #217 shipped — once the call-history audit trail was visible (the #217 fix), the operator noticed two pre-existing banner UX gaps: (a) "muted by default" — the post-Accept banner showed a "Mute" button label which the operator read as "I'm currently muted" even though `track.enabled` was `true`; (b) "disconnect button invisible" — the disconnect CTA used `bg-red-650` which is NOT in the Tailwind palette so the button rendered with no background. **Fix — three-part change.** **(1) Live mic status pill always visible** — the pre-#218 indicator was `hidden lg:flex` (desktop only). The fix replaces it with an always-visible pill (no `hidden` class): coloured dot (`bg-emerald-500` for open, `bg-red-500` for muted) + state label ("Mic open" / "Mic muted") + `emerald-200/50/700` and `red-200/50/700` border/fill/text combinations. Carries `data-testid="call-mic-status-pill-{roomId}"`. The mute toggle button keeps the action label ("Mute" / "Unmute") so the two surfaces don't compete — together they read as "Mic open · Mute" / "Mic muted · Unmute". **(2) Disconnect button color fix** — `bg-red-650` → `bg-red-600` + `hover:bg-red-700`. Matches the rest of the app's red palette (ReportsPage refund cells, SettingsPage delete buttons). **(3) Mute button title rephrasing** — the unmuted title was "Mute mic. The guest will not hear you while muted." (outcome description); the fix is "Mic is open. The guest can hear you. Click to mute." which matches the muted-state title's "state + click-to-toggle" pattern. **What the user sees after the change** — on Accept, the banner shows a green "Mic open" pill + the blue "Mute" button + a clearly-red Disconnect button. The operator can see the live mic state at a glance on every breakpoint. The Disconnect button is visually red and obviously a CTA. **Implementation** — `admin-app/src/pages/IntercomInboxPage.tsx` (the `hidden lg:flex` indicator replaced with the always-visible pill + the disconnect button className fixed + the title attribute rephrased). No new components, no new dependencies, no Tailwind config changes. **Tests** — 16 new source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-banner-mute-state.test.ts` (3 describe blocks pinning the bug #1 fixes, the bug #2 fixes, the bug #3 rephrasing, plus 4 regression-sanity pins for the pre-existing mute + disconnect surfaces). Plus 2 updates to the pre-existing `feature-intercom-audio-routing.test.ts` to match the new banner shape (the "Voice connected indicators" comment anchor is now "Live mic status pill"; the "Audio Stream: Active" + "Mic Muted" text pins are now "Mic open" + "Mic muted"). All 1406 admin-app tests pass. **MDs** — `admin-app/src/pages/IntercomInboxPage.tsx`, `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-banner-mute-state.test.ts` (NEW), `admin-app/src/__tests__/feature-intercom-audio-routing.test.ts` (2 test updates), `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #218`, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry). **Gates** — INTERCOM-INBOX (the three-state banner from #214/#215/#216/#217 is unchanged; the #218 changes are in the active-call sub-banner), INTERCOM-GUEST (guest side unaffected), INTERCOM-AUDIO-ROUTING (audio device routing unchanged; the #218 changes are visual-only), FOL-03 (no new transactional surface), NC-02d (no new rule surface). **Rejected** — rename the button label to "Mute mic" / "Unmute mic" so the action is unambiguous on its own (would partially help but the root cause is the MISSING state cue, not the action label — the pill + button together is the symmetric fix), add a tooltip that always shows the current state on hover (tooltips are passive and require hover — operators on touch devices don't get hover, and the operator needs the state at a glance, not on hover), make the button always red regardless of state (defeats the purpose of the colour-coded state pill — green + red together is the conferencing-app convention for "you're live vs you're muted"), use a modal popover with the mic controls (a modal for a 1-click toggle is massive friction — operators mute/unmute frequently during calls), also fix the other `bg-red-650` instances scattered across `BookingsPage.tsx` + `SettingsPage.tsx` + `ReportsPage.tsx` + `DashboardPage.tsx` (each instance is its own operator-visible bug but they were not flagged by this operator report — a future audit pass could file them as a separate decision entry; the #218 fix is scoped to the call banner where the operator reported the bug), pin only the `bg-red-650` absence and not the `bg-red-600` presence (the absence pin catches the bug, the presence pin catches a future refactor that drops the disconnect button entirely from the active-call banner — both pins are needed for the contract), make the pill a `position: fixed` overlay so it doesn't take up banner space (the pill is small + the banner already has room for the three surfaces — a fixed overlay would obscure the chat thread on small screens), bump the version MAJOR (the change is purely visual — no API contract change, no new field, no new wire shape; `fix:` per Conventional Commits triggers a PATCH bump per `Husky` auto-versioning).

- ✅ **Mic mute state derives from live `track.enabled`, not from React `isMicMuted` (operator-reported bug fix, 2026-08-19)** (shipped 2026-08-19 on `fix/call-banner-mic-state-derived-from-track`, per decision #219; v0.274.5 → v0.274.6). Operator-reported 2026-08-19 immediately after #218 — the pill said "MIC OPEN" but the audio was muted. Root cause: the displayed state (`isMicMuted` useState) and the actual audio (`track.enabled`) had drifted apart — something other than `toggleMicMute` had flipped the track (likely WebRTC renegotiation swapping the outbound track, or a stale closure in an early render). Whatever the cause, the operator could not see what the guest was hearing. **Fix — four-part change.** **(1) `getActualMicMuted()` helper** added at `AdminContext.tsx:3840-3856` — reads `adminMediaStreamRef.current.getAudioTracks()[0].enabled` and returns the negation. Idle state (no stream) returns `false`. The function is a `useCallback([])` so it's referentially stable across renders. **(2) `toggleMicMute` rewritten** to derive `nextEnabled` from the live `tracks[0].enabled` rather than from the React `isMicMuted` state. The `useCallback` dep array is now `[]` because the function no longer reads any React state — only the ref + the track. **(3) `AdminContextType` interface + return value gain `getActualMicMuted: () => boolean`** so the banner can subscribe. `isMicMuted` stays on the type + return value as a hint for any future consumer (analytics, mute-by-default toggle). **(4) `IntercomInboxPage` active-call IIFE captures `actualMicMuted` at the top of the render** (`const actualMicMuted = getActualMicMuted();`) and uses it for the pill text, pill border/fill/text, pill dot, button label, button icon, button styling, button `aria-pressed`, button `aria-label`, and button `title` — every visual + accessibility surface. The `useAdmin()` destructure intentionally does NOT pull `isMicMuted`. **What the user sees after the change** — the pill, button label, button icon, button styling, button aria-pressed, and button title all reflect the LIVE `track.enabled` value. The pill, button, and audio can never disagree. **Implementation** — `admin-app/src/context/AdminContext.tsx` (~30 lines) + `admin-app/src/pages/IntercomInboxPage.tsx` (~5 lines). **Tests** — 25 new source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-banner-mic-state-track-truth.test.ts` (3 describe blocks: `getActualMicMuted` reads the live track state, `toggleMicMute` uses the live track state as its flip source, `isMicMuted` remains as a hint not the display source, IntercomInboxPage derives display from `actualMicMuted`). Plus 3 test updates in `feature-intercom-audio-routing.test.ts` + 2 test updates in `call-banner-mute-state.test.ts` to match the new shape. All 1431 admin-app tests pass. **MDs** — `admin-app/src/context/AdminContext.tsx`, `admin-app/src/pages/IntercomInboxPage.tsx`, `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-banner-mic-state-track-truth.test.ts` (NEW), `admin-app/src/__tests__/feature-intercom-audio-routing.test.ts`, `admin-app/src/__tests__/call-banner-mute-state.test.ts`, `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #219`, `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (this entry). **Gates** — INTERCOM-INBOX (banner shape from #218 is unchanged; #219 changes only the SOURCE of the state the banner reads), INTERCOM-GUEST (guest side unaffected), INTERCOM-AUDIO-ROUTING (audio device routing unchanged), FOL-03 (no new transactional surface), NC-02d (no new rule surface). **Rejected** — keep the `useState` source-of-truth + add a `useEffect` polling loop (wasteful for an event-driven surface; the IIFE capture on every render already gives the live value), read `track.enabled` inside a `useEffect` and force-sync `isMicMuted` (would re-introduce the dual-source problem and add a render-cycle lag), drop the React `isMicMuted` state entirely (wider blast radius than this fix needs), move the mic-mute toggle into a separate component (over-engineering for one banner section), use Zustand for the call state (out of scope for a fix-this-bug ticket), bump the version MAJOR (the change is purely about which signal the banner reads — no API contract change; `fix:` triggers a PATCH bump per `Husky` auto-versioning).

- ⏸ **`recordCallHistory` write failures should surface as a `notify.error` toast, not a silent `console.warn`** *(filed 2026-08-19 from decision #216)* — `AdminContext.recordCallHistory` wraps every call-history write (`intercoms/{roomId}/messages` addDoc) in a `try/catch` that logs `[call-history] failed to record ${outcome} for ${roomId}: ...` as a `console.warn` and moves on (per `AdminContext.tsx:3576-3580`). The pre-#216 bug surfaced exactly because of this shape: the rule rejected the write, the warn was silent from the staff member's perspective, and the thread never received the "Call answered" message — the operator only found out by manually inspecting the thread. The #216 rule fix unblocks the write for the common case, but any FUTURE write failure (Firestore quota, network blip, a new rule field mismatch, the `intercoms/{roomId}/messages` parent doc missing, etc.) would still go to console without a UI signal. **Scope when picked up** — ~5 lines in `recordCallHistory`: replace the silent `console.warn` with `notify.error("Call history not recorded", "The call-history entry for room {roomId} could not be saved. See the browser console for details.")` (the toast pattern matches the existing `acceptCall` "Call taken" toast on `AdminContext.tsx:4004-4010`). The toast should be inside the `try/catch` so the failure path can deliver it; the `console.warn` should stay as the secondary diagnostic signal (toast for staff, console for the operator's devtools). **Re-evaluate if** the operator reports another "missing call history on the thread" incident; the toast would have surfaced the failure in the staff's view instead of requiring a devtools dig. **Follow-up priority** — low (the #216 rule fix unblocks the common case; this is a defense-in-depth improvement, not a current bug). Pair with a source-text test that pins `recordCallHistory` calls `notify.error` on catch.
